# BT23 audit

## Status

All 102 BT23 cards carry current, independently reproduced 10/10 evidence: 1020/1020 points on the 2026-09-10 re-audit ledger, delivered by commits b152f9558 (catalog), 3c38ddf51 (engine test) and 714a9f9e0 (cards and synchronized effect records) on `origin/audit-bt23-fable-20260910`. This resolves the contradiction the set carried until now. The 2026-09-07 ledger (last commit 60abbf7d9) recorded 890/1020 with every card capped at 9/10 because delivery ran on an uncommitted tree, while the 2026-09-06 `BT23-AUDIT.md` claimed 102/102 and disclaimed itself. Neither score was inherited. This re-audit started from base `6dba7443b`, re-derived every printed clause against the catalog and the rulings knowledge base, and replaced the proof style that the 2026-09-07 run had left in place: 15 test files that proved clauses with injected timing, 59 that wrote `turnSeat` directly, 12 with numeric `security: n` fixtures (which the harness fills with a Digi-Egg) and 4 with Digi-Egg cards seeded in decks or security stacks. Every card now proves its clauses through public intents on the real turn loop, with one lane report per card reproduced by the coordinator. Fourteen modules received production IR corrections (listed under Open items), two catalog strings were corrected, and no engine code changed: the two engine seams the lanes raised were reproduced and shown to be fixture defects. The `@ts-nocheck` removal requested for this set was already complete at base; the static guard (0 suppressions, 0 casts, 102 exclusive `registerIrCard` registrations) is part of the closing gates. The 2026-09-10 re-audit is the winning source; both earlier documents are superseded.

## Gates

Commands and results below are from the 2026-09-10 re-audit closeout, run by the coordinator on the worktree `audit-bt23-fable-20260910` at commit 714a9f9e0 (base `6dba7443b6e57afefb7f6c4d1da84a3e62731e3d`). Every focused suite was reproduced by the coordinator after its lane reported; the per-card result lines are in the Card ledger.

| Gate | Command | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Baseline collection (before any edit) | `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23 --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` | 103 files, 1570 tests passed |
| Baseline manifest | `pnpm effects:check:set -- --set BT23 --base 6dba7443b` | 0 semantic changes, 102 records synchronized |
| Collection | `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23 --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` | 103 files, 1652 tests passed, 0 failed, 0 expected-fail markers |
| Mechanism | `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/engine/conformance src/engine/combat src/engine/effects src/engine/cards src/cards/audit-docs.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` | 139 files, 2075 tests passed |
| Engine lane regression (before the cards commit) | same mechanism command plus the BT23-017/027/032/050 suites | 138 files / 2071 tests and 4 files / 80 tests passed; no production engine change |
| Catalog sync | `pnpm effects:sync:set -- --set BT23 --base 6dba7443b` then `pnpm effects:check:set -- --set BT23 --base 6dba7443b` | 12 semantic BT23 changes, zero semantic or byte changes outside the set, 102 records synchronized |
| Typecheck | `pnpm typecheck` | exit 0, 0 errors |
| Lint | `pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/BT23` | exit 0, 0 errors, 149 warnings (`no-explicit-any` on IR-shape assertions, `vitest/no-conditional-expect`) |
| Format | `pnpm exec oxfmt --check apps/api/src/cards/BT23 apps/api/src/engine/combat` | clean; the only hit under `packages/shared/src/effects` (`dnaDigivolutionCoverage.test.ts`) is byte-identical to base |
| Static guards | grep over `apps/api/src/cards/BT23` | 102/102 exclusive `registerIrCard`, 0 `registerCard`, 0 `@ts-nocheck`/`@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error`, 0 `as any` casts in modules, 0 `it.fails`/`it.skip`, 0 files with injected timing, numeric security, Digi-Egg deck/security fixtures or `turnSeat` writes |
| Diff check | `git diff --check` | clean |
| Delivery | commits `b152f9558`, `3c38ddf51`, `714a9f9e0` pushed to `origin/audit-bt23-fable-20260910` | local and remote SHA `714a9f9e02f9f48890328078ffb379a2e192b548`; delivery gates awarded collection-wide after the push |

Machine guardrails during the run: 16 GiB RAM, at most four opus card lanes plus one serialized engine lane, every vitest run single-worker without file parallelism, a 30-second memory/disk/vitest-count monitor (free memory never dropped below 43%; free disk fell to 1.0 GiB once from unrelated machine activity and recovered after regenerable build outputs were removed).

### BT23-001 — Flickmon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`: Blue DigiEgg, Lv2, forms `["Appmon"]`, types `["Flick"]`,
inherited text `[When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] If this Digimon has the [Appmon] trait, ＜Draw 1＞`. No main text.
`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-001` → "(no knowledge-base entries)". KB-INDEX lists no Q&A ids, so none to cover.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[When Attacking]` inherited trigger | `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, `isInherited: true` | "draws for an Appmon carrier, only once in the turn" — public `attack` intent, hand +1 (public flow) |
| `If this Digimon has the [Appmon] trait` | `condition.selfHasTrait` with `match: "trait"`, token `Appmon` | positive over BT23-007 Musclemon (Appmon form); negative "does not draw when the carrier lacks the Appmon trait" over BT1-010 Agumon (public flow) |
| `＜Draw 1＞` | `Draw` amount 1, `controller: "mine"` | hand/deck counts asserted on both sides of every attack (public flow) |
| `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | same-turn refusal (second attack draws nothing) and reset on the next own turn via `startTurnLoop` + `waitForMainPhase` (public flow); per-source independence proved with two Flickmon carriers |
| Digi-Egg identity (evolution source) | catalog only | "publicly evolves an Appmon egg from breeding into off-color Musclemon" — Blue egg into Red BT23-007 through its `[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[Appmon] trait: Cost 0` alternate route, memory unchanged at 0, stack identity asserted (public flow) |

#### Corrections
None. The module and test already met the standard; I reproduced every claim.

#### Commands and results
`pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-001.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism`
→ `Test Files  1 passed (1)` / `Tests  6 passed (6)`.
oxlint: clean. oxfmt --check: correct format. `git diff --check`: clean. `tsc --noEmit`: clean (whole api package).

#### Retained reds and gaps
None. One arranging call remains: `advance(s.engine).verb.unsuspend(...)` between the two attacks in the
once-per-turn test — no public intent unsuspends a Digimon mid-turn, and it only arranges the second attack;
the assertion it supports (no second draw) is still produced by a public `attack`.

#### Catalog discrepancies
None. The catalog uses a non-breaking space in `[Appmon] trait`; the test matches it exactly.

### BT23-008 — Greymon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog (`cardId: "BT23-008"`): Red Digimon, Lv.4, play cost 6, DP 5000,
evoCosts Red/Lv.3/2 and Blue/Lv.3/2, forms `["Champion"]`, attribute Vaccine,
types `["Dinosaur","CS"]`.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Agumon] in name or w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` (two alternates).
2. `＜Raid＞`.
3. `[Main] [Once Per Turn] By placing this Digimon's top stacked card as its bottom digivolution
   card, you may play 1 [Gabumon] or [Nokia Shiramine] from your hand with the play cost reduced by 2.`
4. Inherited: `[Your Turn] This Digimon gets +2000 DP.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-008` returns the four KB-INDEX ids:
- **Q5216** — can I place this card as the bottom digivolution card and then play a specified card? "Yes."
- **Q5217** — with no digivolution cards, can I activate the [Main] effect? "No, you can't activate it."
- **Q5218** — only Digi-Egg cards underneath: activation is legal; this card is placed under them,
  then a specified card may be played; the now-top card with no DP is trashed by rule checks.
- **Q5219** — only a 1000-DP card underneath and another effect reduces DP by 1000: activation is
  legal; if the new top card's DP is 0 it is deleted by rule checks.

Q5216/Q5218/Q5219 confirm the cost places **Greymon itself** (the top card of its own stack) at the
bottom, which is what `cost.target = {isSelfRef:true, isSelf:true}` encodes.

Optional-cost ruling (CR 15-8-4-4-1): once the `[Main]` activation is declared, the "by placing"
cost is **mandatory**; only the reduced-cost play is the "you may" branch. The module encodes this
as `cost: {kind:"place", ...}` with `optional:false` on the action and `target.upTo: true` on the
play (so selecting zero cards is legal but the cost is already spent). The Q5219 wording — "After
activation, you place this card under the digivolution cards, **then** you can play a specified
card" — is the ruling-side confirmation of the same ordering.

Grammar check: "w/[Agumon] in name" is a SUBSTRING match. The IR field `names` is documented in
`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/requirements/digivolve.ts` as "SUBSTRING name match", and
`namesExact` is the exact variant — so `names: ["Agumon"]` is correct here. Conversely the played
card, "1 [Gabumon] or [Nokia Shiramine]", is an EXACT bracketed name and uses `match: "nameExact"`.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Two `[Digivolve]` alternates, cost 2 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:3, names:["Agumon"], cost:2, isAlternate:true}, {level:3, traits:["CS"], cost:2, isAlternate:true}]` | "supports both alternate level-3 boundaries and rejects an off-color nonmatch" (:281) — public `digivolve` over both bases BT11-046 and BT22-017, memory 3→1 each; negative on BT1-064 → `{ok:false, reason:"invalid-evolution"}`. Public flow. |
| `＜Raid＞` | `effects[0]`: `Static` + `keywords:[{keyword:"Raid"}]` | "uses Raid to redirect a player attack to an unsuspended opposing Digimon" (:312) — public `attack` at the player; the opposing BT1-009 is deleted and the opponent's security is untouched at length 1. Public flow. |
| `[Main] [Once Per Turn]` restack cost → may play Gabumon / Nokia at −2 | `effects[1]`: `Main`, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`, `PlayWithoutCost{target:{nameOrTrait:[{tokens:["Gabumon","Nokia Shiramine"],match:"nameExact"}], count:1, upTo:true}, from:["hand"], payCost:true, reduceCostBy:2, cost:{kind:"place", target:{isSelfRef:true,isSelf:true}}, optional:false, abortOnDecline:true}` | Q5216: "pays the restack cost, then plays Gabumon for exactly 2 less" (:86) — public `activateEffect`; memory ends at exactly 4 from 5 (BT1-029's printed play cost 3 reduced by 2), Greymon's own instance ends at `stack[0]` (the bottom) and the previously-stacked card is the new top. Q5217: "cannot declare the Main effect without a digivolution card" (:116) — `{ok:false}`, Gabumon stays in hand, memory unchanged. Q5218: "pays the cost with only a Digi-Egg underneath, then rule-checks the exposed egg" (:134) — the played card arrives, the original permanent is gone, and both Greymon and the egg land in trash. Q5219: "can pay with a 1000 DP source and rule-deletes it when its total DP reaches 0" (:166). All public `activateEffect`. |
| Cost is mandatory once declared (CR 15-8-4-4-1) | `optional:false` on the action; the "may" lives only in `upTo:true` on the play target | "still pays and restacks when the optional reduced play is declined" (:200) — the `selectCards` prompt is answered with `instanceIds: []` and the test asserts the stack DID change, `stack[0]` is Greymon (cost paid), Nokia is still in hand, and memory is unchanged at 5. This is the correct encoding: nothing in the suite blesses declining the cost after declaration. Public flow. |

#### Corrections
Module `BT23-012.ts`: the `(other than [Sea Animal])` parenthetical narrows the `[Animal]` substring
reading only. The old IR put a card-level `excludeNameOrTrait: [Sea Animal]` on the whole animal-family
branch, so a card carrying both `[Sea Animal]` and `[Beast]` was wrongly rejected even though it has
`[Beast]` in its traits. Split into three `or` branches: `[CS]` exact; `traitContains` of
`Avian/Bird/Beast/Sovereign` with no exclusion; `traitContains` of `Animal` with the `[Sea Animal]`
exclusion. Applied to both the top-card and the inherited effect.

`s.state.turnSeat = 1` battle test to one public `battleDeleteHost` helper (attack into security to
suspend, then lose a battle on the opponent's turn); swapped the inherited host from `BT23-013` to the
effect-free `BT1-014`; updated the IR assertion and added the `[Sea Animal] + [Beast]` case, which now
matches.

#### Retained reds and gaps
Residual, documented in the module comment: no filter primitive excludes a single trait from a substring
scan, so a card carrying BOTH `[Sea Animal]` and another `[...Animal]` spelling (for example
`[Dark Animal]`) is still rejected. No printed card has that trait pair — a catalog sweep for
`Sea Animal` plus any of `Avian/Bird/Beast/Sovereign` returns none — so the gap is unreachable in play.
Closing it fully needs a per-trait exclusion primitive in the shared filter (coordinator-owned).

### BT23-014 — Gallantmon

#### Contract and rules evidence
- Catalog: `BT23-014`. Red Lv.6 Virus, play 11, DP 11000, traits [Holy Warrior]/[Royal Knight]/[CS].
- Printed clauses: (1) `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`; (2) `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Until
  your opponent's turn ends, their effects can't play Digimon or Tamers from the trash.`; (3) `[On Play]
  [When Digivolving] [When Attacking] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with 8000 DP or less. For each
  of their Digimon and Tamers, add 2000 to this DP deletion effect's maximum.`
- KB: Q5225 (the two entry effects trigger simultaneously; the player orders them), Q5226 (the floodgate
  affects the opponent player's effects), Q5227 (your own effect may still play their Digimon from their
  trash), Q5228 (their effect may not play your Digimon from the trash), Q5229 (the ceiling arithmetic),
  Q6249 (their effects also cannot play into their breeding area). All six covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Alternate route Lv.5 w/[CS], cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | public: `digivolves for 3 from an off-color level-5 CS Digimon and rejects an off-color non-CS base` (L293), memory 3 -> 0 and `invalid-evolution` on the negative |
| Trash floodgate, opponent effects only, until their turn ends | two effects (`OnPlay`, `WhenDigivolving`) each with `RestrictPlay {seat:"opponent", filter:{kind:["Digimon","Tamer"], zone:"trash"}, mode:"play", duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd", byEffectOnly:true}` | public: `expires the public trash-play lock at the end of the opponent's turn` (L229) — real turn loop, a BT11-086 DigiXros play is blocked from the trash on the locked turn and succeeds on the next one; structural complement `scopes the trash floodgate...` (L175) covers Q5226-Q5228 and Q6249 (breeding destination) through the continuous ledger and the play verbs, which is the only route to an opponent effect playing into their own breeding area |
| Two entry effects are separate and player-ordered (Q5225) | two distinct `OnPlay` / `WhenDigivolving` entries, not merged | IR assertion (L10) plus the public play test, which resolves both under `autoOrderTriggers` |
| Multi-step [CS] evolution stack | `digivolutionRequirement` applied at each rung | public: `climbs a public Lv.3 -> Lv.4 -> Lv.5 [CS] stack into Gallantmon, drawing once per step` (L293) — see Follow-ups |
| Delete <=8000, +2000 per opponent Digimon and Tamer (Q5229) | `Delete` with `dpCeilingScaling {per:1, amount:2000, unit:"cards", filter:{controller:"opponent", kind:["Digimon","Tamer"], zone:"battleArea"}}` | public: `deletes at the scaled 12000-DP ceiling from a public play` (L61), `...from a public CS digivolution` (L85), `...from a public attack but spares 13000` (L119, both boundary endpoints), and `keeps the 8000 base ceiling when the opponent fields nothing else` (L157) |

#### Follow-up (coordinator round 2): peer and evolution-stack proof
Two new public tests in `BT23-014.test.ts`:

1. `climbs a public Lv.3 -> Lv.4 -> Lv.5 [CS] stack into Gallantmon, drawing once per step` (L293).
   Veemon `BT22-019` is PLAYED from hand, then digivolved three times through the `[CS]` alternate
   route (`useAlternateCost: true` on every rung): `BT22-022` Veedramon (Lv.4 [CS]), `BT22-023`
   AeroVeedramon (Lv.5 [CS]), then `BT23-014`. The whole line is blue, so the red normal route is
   unavailable and each rung can only be the `[CS]` recipe. Asserted at every step:
   - source-stack identity — `stack.map(cardId)` equals the exact bottom-most-first source list
     (`[BT22-019]`, then `[BT22-019, BT22-022]`, then `[BT22-019, BT22-022, BT22-023]`);
   - the new top card's id and its recomputed `currentDP` (5000 / 7000 / 11000);
   - the bonus draw — the deck loses exactly one card per rung and the net hand size is unchanged
     (one card spent digivolving, one drawn);
   - cost 3 on the Gallantmon rung (memory 8 -> 5).
   The opponent fields one effect-free level-6 body, so AeroVeedramon's mandatory "return 1 level 4 or
   lower" finds no target and the board can only change through Gallantmon's own entry deletion — which
   then clears the 10000-DP body at the one-permanent scaled ceiling, proving the [When Digivolving]
   face is live at the top of a real stack.

2. `accepts only the $label host for the cost-3 [CS] route` (L358), a mixed Lv.5 host pool run through
   the public digivolve intent:
   - `BT22-023` AeroVeedramon (Lv.5, exact `[CS]`, blue) — ACCEPTED, memory 6 -> 3, source in stack;
   - `BT17-036` Boutmon (Lv.5, yellow/green, traits `[Beastkin]`/`[Abadin Electronics]`) — REJECTED.
     `Abadin Electronics` is the catalog's only trait that CONTAINS "cs" without being `[CS]`, so this
     is the exact substring-versus-exact discriminator the trait gate needs;
   - `BT1-041` Zudomon (Lv.5, blue, `[Sea Beast]`) — REJECTED, unrelated trait;
   - `BT22-022` Veedramon (Lv.4, `[CS]`) — REJECTED, wrong level;
   - `BT22-013` WarGreymon (Lv.6, `[CS]`) — REJECTED, wrong level.
   Every rejected case asserts the base's top card, the Gallantmon card still in hand, and memory
   unchanged at 6.

Focused suite after the follow-up: `Tests  14 passed (14)`. Typecheck rerun: clean.

#### Contract and rules evidence

  [When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with 9000 DP or less. Then, you

  Place this card face up as the bottom security card.`

  [On Deletion]), Q5231 (face-up security cards stay revealed), Q5232 (a check runs normally on a
  revealed card), Q5233 (a [Security] effect still triggers on a face-up card), Q5234 (shuffling turns
  every face-up security card face down). All five covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Alternate route Lv.5 w/[CS], cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | public: `digivolves for 3 from an off-color level-5 CS Digimon and rejects an off-color non-CS base` (L503) |
| Cost reduction of 5 with a [Zaxon] Tamer | `Static -> Replacement {event:"wouldBePlayed", sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}}` wrapping `Replacement {mode:"reduceCost", amount:5, condition:{kind:"youHave", filter:{kind:["Tamer"], nameOrTrait:[{tokens:["Zaxon"], match:"trait"}]}}}` | public: `pays exactly 6 with a Zaxon Tamer and cannot complete the unreduced play at 0 memory` (L83) — memory 10 -> 4 with `BT23-086` Yuugo out, and the unreduced play leaves hand and board untouched |
| Delete 1 opponent Digimon <=9000 DP | three effects (`OnPlay`/`WhenDigivolving`/`WhenAttacking`) each `Delete` with `dp {op:"lte", value:9000}` | public: `deletes at exactly 9000 DP...` (L109, public play), `resolves the shared deletion effect from a public attack` (L136), `shares the public deletion use across evolution and attack` (L339) |
| "Then, you may return up to 3 non-Digi-Egg cards from their trash to the bottom of the deck" | `Return` with `zone:"trash"`, `controller:"opponent"`, `kind:["Digimon","Tamer","Option"]` (this is how "non-Digi-Egg" is expressed), `count:3`, `upTo:true`, `to:"deckBottom"`, `optional:true` | public: `returns at most 3 non-Digi-Egg cards to deck bottom even when no deletion occurs` (L202, exact deck-bottom order, the fourth card and the Digi-Egg left in trash), refusal `allows the optional trash return to be refused without moving cards` (L234) |
| Q5230: the returned card's pending [On Deletion] cannot activate | ordering of the two actions inside one effect | public: `deletes at exactly 9000 DP, then returns that card before its pending On Deletion can activate` (L109) — the deleted BT23-012 ends at deck bottom, not in trash, and its [On Deletion] never plays the Birdramon from hand |
| `[Once Per Turn]` shared across all three timings | `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` plus `sharedUseKey:"ir-shared-0"` on all three effects | public: `resets the shared public attack effect on the next own turn` (L295) and `shares the public deletion use across evolution and attack, then resets next own turn` (L339), both through the real turn loop with intermediate state asserted; `tracks the shared Once Per Turn per source: a second Phoenixmon still deletes` (L252) proves the use key is per source |
| `[On Deletion]` place face up as the bottom security card | `SecurityManipulation {op:"placeAsSecurity", controller:"mine", toTop:false, faceUp:true}` | public: `publicly places itself face up at security bottom when deleted in battle` (L157) — real turn loop, attacked and deleted on the opponent's turn, lands last in the stack with `faceUp: true` |
| Q5231/Q5232 face-up security behaviour | engine security rules | public: `checks a face-up security card with the card left revealed` (L414) |
| Q5233 [Security] effect on a face-up card | engine security rules | public: `fires a [Security] effect from a face-up security card on its check` (L449) — a face-up `P-066` is checked and its [Security] deletion removes the 3000-DP attacker |
| Q5234 shuffle turns face-up cards face down | engine security rules | public: `puts a face-up security card back face down when the stack is shuffled` (L479) — `BT1-087` T.K. played from hand |

Public flow, no injected timing. |

Public flow. |

### BT23-018 — Garurumon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog (`cardId: "BT23-018"`): Blue Digimon, Lv.4, play cost 6, DP 5000,
`evoCosts [{Blue,3,2},{Red,3,2}]`, forms `["Champion"]`, attribute Vaccine,
types `["Beast","CS"]`, `inheritedEffectText: "[Opponent's Turn] This Digimon gets +2000 DP."`.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Gabumon] in name or w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` (alternate).
2. `＜Jamming＞`.
3. `[Main] [Once Per Turn] By placing this Digimon's top stacked card as its bottom digivolution
   card, you may play 1 [Agumon] or [Nokia Shiramine] from your hand with the play cost reduced
   by 2.`
4. Inherited `[Opponent's Turn] This Digimon gets +2000 DP.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-018` returns Q5236, Q5237, Q5238, Q5239 — three covered
behaviorally, Q5239 documented.

Grammar check: "[Gabumon] in name" is an explicit substring gate, so `names: ["Gabumon"]`
(substring mode) is correct; "[CS] trait" is exact; the bracketed `[Agumon]` / `[Nokia Shiramine]`
play targets use `match: "nameExact"`. All correct.

Optional-cost ruling (CR 15-8-4-4-1): once the `[Main]` effect is declared, the restack cost is
mandatory; only the reduced-cost play is optional. The module encodes exactly that — the
`PlayWithoutCost` action carries `optional: false` with `target.upTo: true`, and its `cost`
(`kind: "place"`, `isSelf: true`) carries no `optional` flag, so it cannot be declined. Confirmed
behaviorally by "still pays and restacks when the optional reduced play is declined": the player
selects zero cards at the play prompt, memory stays at 5, Nokia stays in hand, and Garurumon has
still moved to stack[0].

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Gabumon] in name or w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:3, names:["Gabumon"], cost:2, isAlternate:true}, {level:3, traits:["CS"], cost:2, isAlternate:true}]` | "publicly evolves from exact off-color Gabumon and CS, while rejecting wrong level and trait" — public `digivolve` from off-color ST16-03 Gabumon and from BT23-037 (CS), each paying exactly 2 with the source at stack[0] and hand size unchanged; two negatives (level-4 BT23-020 source, non-CS BT1-064 source) with memory, hand and top card unchanged. Public flow. |
| `＜Jamming＞` | `effects[0]`: `Static` with `keywords: [{keyword:"Jamming"}]` | "keeps Jamming Garurumon after a larger security battle but loses in an ordinary larger battle" — public `attack` into a 6000 DP security Digimon leaves Garurumon on the field and out of the trash with security at 0; the same attack against a suspended 6000 DP battle-area Digimon deletes Garurumon and leaves the defender. Public flow. |
| `[Main] [Once Per Turn] By placing this Digimon's top stacked card as its bottom digivolution card…` (cost) | `Main` effect, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`; `cost:{kind:"place", target:{filter:{isSelfRef:true}, count:1, isSelf:true}}`, no `optional` | "pays the restack cost, then plays Agumon for exactly 2 less, per Q5236" — public `activateEffect`, memory 5→4 for a cost-3 Agumon (3 − 2 = 1), top card becomes the previously exposed source, Garurumon sits at stack[0]. Q5237: "cannot declare without a digivolution card" — `activateEffect` on a sourceless Garurumon returns `{ok:false}`. Q5238: new "activates with only a Digi-Egg beneath…" — the activation is legal, the reduced play still happens, and the DP-less Digi-Egg top card takes the whole permanent to the trash by rule check (both the egg and Garurumon end in the trash). Public flow. |
| "…you may play 1 [Agumon] or [Nokia Shiramine] … with the play cost reduced by 2" | `PlayWithoutCost{ from:["hand"], payCost:true, reduceCostBy:2, target: nameExact ["Agumon","Nokia Shiramine"], count 1, upTo:true }` | Exact-name boundary: "offers exact Agumon while excluding Agumon X Antibody" — the `selectCards` candidate list contains BT1-010 Agumon and not BT9-008 Agumon (X Antibody); selecting Agumon plays it and leaves the X Antibody in hand. Optional refusal proved by the zero-selection test above. Public flow. |
| `[Once Per Turn]` identity | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | New "caps the Main effect at once per turn per source and keeps a second Garurumon's use available" — first activation resolves, a second `activateEffect` on the same source in the same turn is refused with the second Agumon still in hand, and a second Garurumon permanent still gets its own use. |
| Inherited `[Opponent's Turn] This Digimon gets +2000 DP` | `effects[2]`: `OpponentsTurn`, `isInherited:true` → `ModifyDP{ isSelfRef, amount:2000, duration:"permanent" }` | New "grants inherited +2000 DP only during the opponent's turn, across real turn boundaries" — real `startTurnLoop()`: BT23-023 host with BT23-018 underneath reads base DP at seat 0's Main, base + 2000 at seat 1's Main, and base again back at seat 0's Main. Public flow. |

#### Corrections
- `BT23-018.test.ts`: replaced the inherited-DP test that set `s.state.turnSeat` directly and called
  `recomputeContinuousEffects()` with a real `startTurnLoop()` version crossing two turn boundaries.
  This removes the file's only direct state mutation for that clause.
- Added the Q5238 proof (Digi-Egg-only digivolution cards) and the once-per-turn cap / per-source
  identity proof.
- Module `BT23-018.ts`: no change needed. The mandatory restack cost and the optional reduced play
  are already encoded per CR 15-8-4-4-1, as required by the coordinator note.

- No `it.fails`, no engine seam gaps.
- A next-own-turn reset of the `[Once Per Turn]` Main effect is **unobservable for this card**: the
  mandatory cost buries Garurumon at the bottom of its own stack, so it is never the top card (and
  never the effect source) again. The test proves the same-turn refusal and per-source
  independence instead, with the reason recorded in a comment.

Printed clauses:

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-019` → "(no knowledge-base entries)". KB-INDEX records no Q&A
and no errata.

Reading of clause 2: "any 2 … from your opponent's Digimon" pools candidates across every opposing
Digimon that has digivolution cards; it is mandatory, and clamps when fewer than two exist.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{level:3, traits:["CS"], cost:2, isAlternate:true}]` | "uses the alternate level-3 CS evolution recipe and rejects a non-CS peer" (BT23-019.test.ts) — public `digivolve` onto red CS BT23-006 (off-colour, so the route can only be the alternate one), memory 2→0, source at stack[0], exact top card; negative on non-CS BT1-064 returns `{ok:false}`. Public flow. |
| `[On Play] … Trash any 2 digivolution cards from your opponent's Digimon` | `effects[0]`: `OnPlay` → `TrashDigivolution{ scope:"acrossDigimon", amount:2, target: opponent Digimon with `digivolutionCards:"hasAny"` }` | "on play trashes exactly two sources from one opposing Digimon" — public `playCard`, target stack emptied, opponent trash length 2. Clamp: "mandatory trash clamps to the one available source" — a single source available, exactly 1 trashed. Exclusions: "does not trash own cards, top cards, or hosts without sources" — own stack intact, sourceless opposing host untouched, both trashes empty. Public flow. |
| `[When Digivolving] …` (same text) | `effects[1]`: identical `TrashDigivolution` under `WhenDigivolving` | "publicly triggers the When Digivolving trash across two opposing hosts" — public `digivolve`, one physical source removed from each of two opposing hosts, exact instance ids asserted in the opponent trash, memory 2→0. Interactive boundary: "publicly exposes exactly three opposing source candidates and accepts two across hosts" — the `selectCards` request has exactly 3 candidates with `min = max = 2`, excludes the controller's own source and the opponent's breeding Digimon, and after answering, the chosen two are in the trash, the unchosen one stays in its stack, and the bonus draw arrives. Public flow. |
| Inherited `＜Blocker＞` | `effects[2]`: `Static`, `isInherited:true`, `keywords:[{keyword:"Blocker"}]` | "uses the inherited Blocker keyword in a real attack window" — real public `attack`, `blockWindowOpened` lists only the host with Gekomon underneath, `declareBlock` suspends it, the attacker is trashed and security is unchanged. New "carries Blocker only as an inherited effect, not on Gekomon itself" — a Gekomon top card has no Blocker keyword and is excluded from `eligibleBlockerIds`, while the host with Gekomon underneath is the sole eligible blocker. Public flow. |

### BT23-023 — Whamon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog: Blue, Lv5, play 9, DP 9000, evoCosts Blue Lv4 cost 3, form Ultimate, attribute Vaccine,
types `Sea Animal`/`CS`.
Printed clauses:
1. [Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3 (alternate digivolution route)
2. [All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon would leave the battle area other than by your
   effects, you may play 1 level 4 or lower blue or [CS] trait Digimon card from its digivolution
   cards without paying the cost.
3. The same text as an inherited effect.
KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-023` → Q5244 (main text plays a level ≤4 BLUE card **or** a
level ≤4 [CS]-trait card — two independent filters, not an intersection) and Q5245 (the inherited
effect has the same reach). Both covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| [Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement:[{level:4,traits:["CS"],cost:3,isAlternate:true}]` | Public `digivolve`: "publicly evolves for 3 from a CS level-4 source and preserves it beneath the new top card" (L214) — memory 3→0, exact `stack` identity, bonus draw. **New** illegal-source negative: "rejects the alternate Digivolve from a level-4 source without the CS trait" (L237) with `useAlternateCost: true` → `ok:false`, memory and hand untouched. |
| [All Turns] [Once Per Turn] would-leave replacement, optional play from own digivolution cards, blue **or** [CS], level ≤4 (Q5244) | `AllTurns` effect, `Replacement` on `wouldLeavePlay` with `leaveCause:"otherThanYourEffect"`, `sourceFilter {isSelfRef:true}`, inner `PlayWithoutCost` with `from:["digivolutionCards"]`, `colors:["Blue"]` plus an `orFilters` [CS]-trait branch | Public opponent effects: "publicly plays a blue level-4 source for free when an opponent Option deletes Whamon" (L83, ST1-16 Gaia Force); "publicly replaces opponent-effect deletion with its exact source card" (L116, BT23-015 Phoenixmon). Source scope: "only searches Whamon's own digivolution cards and skips an ineligible source" (L292) — a neighbour's stack is untouched and the non-blue non-CS source goes to the trash. Optional refusal: **new** "may decline the optional replacement play and let the source go to the trash" (L361). Once-per-turn and reset: "plays the exact Whamon source when Decoy protects its host from public Gaia Force" (L142) — a second same-turn Gaia Force triggers nothing, and after a full turn cycle a third one fires the reaction again. |
| Owner-effect exclusion ("other than by your effects") | `leaveCause:"otherThanYourEffect"` | "does not react to its controller's own effect" (L393) — the source stays in the trash. Structural (`advance().verb.deletePermanent`), because no public own-effect deletes your own Digimon without carrying text of its own; stated in the test comment. |
| Inherited copy (Q5245) | second `AllTurns` effect with `isInherited:true`, identical action | Public: "uses inherited Whamon replacement when an opponent effect removes a legal stacked host" (L258, Phoenixmon) and "inherits the same CS-or-blue reaction from a realistic stack, per Q5245" (L330) — a 4-card BT23-035 stack carrying Whamon plays the [CS]-trait Betamon source when Gaia Force deletes the carrier. |

#### Corrections
Module: none. Test file substantially rewritten:
- Removed every `s.state.turnSeat = …` write (7 of them). All opponent-turn tests now reach seat 1
  through a `toOpponentMain` helper that runs the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop` →
  `waitForMainPhase(0)` → `endMainPhaseIfOpen(0)` → `waitForMainPhase(1)`).
- Removed all Digi-Egg cards (`BT1-001`..`BT1-008`) from `deck` fixtures; replaced with a shared
  `INERT_DECK` of main-deck Digimon (`BT1-009`..`BT1-014`, `BT1-027`, `BT1-028`).
- Replaced three `advance().verb.deletePermanent` structural tests with public opponent plays of
  ST1-16 Gaia Force ("[Main] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon"), each with a red enabler on the
  opponent board and `preferInstanceIds` naming the intended target.
- Added the optional-refusal test and the illegal-digivolution-source negative.
- Removed the now-unused `observe` import.
Note for other lanes: `const loop = await toOpponentMain(s)` must not return a bare promise — an
`await` on a promise that resolves to a promise chains onto the turn loop and hangs the test. The
helper returns `{ loop }`.

#### Contract and rules evidence

attribute God, type Invincible. No `linkRequirement`/`linkDp` (it is a link host, not a link card).

2. ＜Evade＞
3. ＜Link +1＞
4. [When Digivolving] [When Attacking] You may link 1 Digimon card with the [Appmon] trait from your
   hand or this Digimon's digivolution cards, without paying the cost. (No Once Per Turn printed.)
5. [All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon gets linked, by unsuspending it, other than their
   highest play cost Digimon, none of your opponent's Digimon can suspend until their turn ends.
KB: Q5246, Q5247, Q5248, Q5249, Q5250, Q5251, Q5252, Q6025, Q6026 — all covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| [App Fusion] [Oujamon] & [Beautymon]: Cost 0 | `appFusionRequirement:[{names:["Oujamon","Beautymon"],cost:0}]` | Public `appFusion` intent: "publicly App Fuses Oujamon and Beautymon into Poseidomon" (L83) — linked Beautymon moves into the stack, memory stays 0, bonus draw, `digivolved`/`appFusion` event, no rejection. |
| ＜Evade＞ | `Static` + `GainKeyword {keyword:"Evade"}`, `duration:"permanent"` | Public opponent attack on the opponent's real turn: `it.each` "accepts / refuses Evade after a public Raid attack" (L670) — `evadePrompt`, `respondEvade`, `evadeResolved`, survival vs. trash. |
| ＜Link +1＞ | `Static` + `GainKeyword {keyword:"Link", amount:1}` | "uses Link +1 capacity for two links and replaces the oldest at the third" (L528). |
| [When Digivolving] / [When Attacking] link 1 [Appmon]-trait card, no cost, no Once Per Turn | two effects, `Link` with `nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Appmon"], match:"trait"}]`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true`, **no** `frequency` | Public attack: "links only the valid Appmon candidate from a public attack" (L249) — the non-Appmon and the opponent-controlled candidate are excluded (Q5246). Stack source: "when digivolving links an Appmon from this Digimon's stack, not another friendly stack" (L814). |
| [All Turns] [Once Per Turn] linked → by unsuspending it, opponent Digimon other than the highest play cost can't suspend until their turn ends | `AllTurns` + `SubTrigger whenLinked` + `ArmSuspendRestriction`, `duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd"`, `cost {kind:"unsuspend", isSelfRef}`, `optional:true`, `abortOnDecline:true` | Cost and exemption: "pays the unsuspend cost and dynamically exempts only the highest play-cost Digimon" (L123, Q5247/Q5248); ties: "exempts every tied highest-cost opponent while restricting the lower-cost Digimon" (L155, Q5249); no-play-cost boards: L187 and L219 (Q6025/Q6026); unpayable cost: "cannot arm the restriction when already unsuspended" (L788); expiry: "publicly enforces the suspension exception and expires it after the opponent turn" (L710); reset: "resets the linked unsuspend reaction on the next own turn" (L584). Reclassification: **rewritten** L280 (a higher-cost play on the opponent's real turn, Q5250), **new** L352 ("reclassifies the exemption after an opponent digivolves into a higher play cost, per Q5251"), and L422 (full production turns plus removal of the highest, Q5252). |

#### Corrections
Module: none. Test:

- That L280 test was also materially weakened by its old fixture: Poseidomon was seeded
  `suspended: true`, so under a real turn loop the Unsuspend phase unsuspends it before Main and the
  "by unsuspending it" cost becomes unpayable — the restriction was never armed and the test only

  opponent's higher-cost play.

attribute Vaccine, types `Fairy`/`CS`.
Printed clauses:
1. [Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3
2. [Hand] [Main] If you have a Digimon or Tamer with the [CS] trait, by paying 5 cost, give 3 of your
   opponent's Digimon ＜Security A. -1＞ until their turn ends. Then, place this card as the top
   security card.
3. [On Play] [When Digivolving] Return 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest level to the hand.
4. [Security] At the end of the battle, play this card without paying the cost. At turn end, delete
   this Digimon.
KB: Q5253, Q5254 (both the condition and the payment gate everything, including the "Then" clause),
Q5563, Q5564 (the Security-played Digimon is deleted at the end of the CURRENT turn, and that
deletion is simultaneous with other end-of-turn processing, so the turn player orders them).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| [Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement:[{level:5,traits:["CS"],cost:3,isAlternate:true}]` | Public `digivolve`: "publicly evolves from a CS level-5 source for exactly 3" (L137) and the layered-stack variant (L160) — exact `stack` order across two sources, bonus draw, memory 5→2. |
| [Hand] [Main] condition + "by paying 5 cost" gate (Q5253/Q5254) | `Main` effect, `isFromHand:true`, effect-level `condition youHave` [CS]-trait Digimon/Tamer, plus a `CostGatedBlock` repeating the condition with `cost {payMemory:5}`, `optional:false`, `abortOnDecline:true` | "gates the entire hand Main behind a CS permanent and one payment of 5, per Q5253-Q5254" (L58) — accepted with a CS Digimon, rejected without one, memory and hand untouched on refusal. Controller scoping across a table of CS/non-CS/opponent-owned Tamers (L100). Unpayable cost: L229. Mandatory cost after declaration (CR 15-8-4-4-1): "pays the mandatory Main cost after declaration" (L247) with `autoDeclineOptional` — memory still 5→0 and the card still reaches security. |
| give 3 opponent Digimon ＜Security A. -1＞ until their turn ends | `GainKeyword {keyword:"SecurityAttack", amount:-1}`, `count:3`, `duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | "applies Security Attack -1 to exactly three opposing Digimon and excludes its own Digimon" (L310) — exactly three of four opponents at -1, the fourth and the controller's own Digimon at 0; then, through the real turn loop, the affected attacker checks zero security on the opponent's turn and the modifier is gone on the next own turn. |
| "Then, place this card as the top security card" | `SecurityManipulation {op:"placeAsSecurity", toTop:true}` | Security order asserted as `[marine, oldTop]` (L100 table) and top-of-security identity at L58, L201, L247. |
| [On Play] [When Digivolving] return 1 lowest-level opponent Digimon | two effects with `Return {superlative:"lowestLevel"}, count:1, to:"hand"` | Public `playCard`: "returns exactly one opposing Digimon tied for the lowest level on play" (L278) — exactly one of two tied lowest returns, the higher-level pair stays. Public `digivolve` variant at L160. |
| [Security] play at end of battle, delete at turn end (Q5563/Q5564) | `Security` effect, `timing:"endOfBattle"`, `SubTrigger whenSecurityBattleEnded` (`once:true`) → `PlayWithoutCost` from trash → `DelayedDeletePlayed { timing: "endOfCurrentTurn" }` | "publicly plays from Security after an opponent attack and deletes only that played Digimon at turn end, per Q5563-Q5564" (L370) — a real opponent attack on the opponent's turn checks security, MarineAngemon is played, wins the battle (attacker trashed, [On Play] returns the other opponent Digimon), is still on the board mid-turn, and is gone by the end of **that** turn, while a second MarineAngemon already on the board is untouched and only one copy is in the trash. |

- Q5257: the whole Hudiemon/Erika double-＜Alliance＞ line — the DNA result arrives **unsuspended**, so the second ＜Alliance＞ can suspend it, and the first ＜Alliance＞'s DP stays on Hudiemon.
- Q6250: with Angemon in Shakkoumon's digivolution cards, ＜Barrier＞ may be accepted first and Shakkoumon's [All Turns] play-from-sources may still run.

### BT23-028 — Coordemon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog (`BT23-028`): Yellow Lv.4, play cost 4, DP 4000, forms `Sup.`/`Appmon`, trait `Coordinate`, `linkDp` 3000, `linkRequirement` `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 2`.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Security] At the end of the battle, play this card without paying the cost.`
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] 1 of your opponent's Digimon gets -3000 DP for the turn.`
3. Link: `[When Linking] Until your opponent's turn ends, 1 of their Digimon can't activate [When Digivolving] effects.`

KB: **Q5258–Q5262**, all about the exact meaning of "can't activate [When Digivolving] effects": it blocks the trigger itself (Q5258), it does not block the same effect activating at a *[When Attacking]* timing (Q5259), no other effect can activate it (Q5260), the "by" condition alone may not be processed (Q5261), and a blocked activation does not consume its `[X Per Turn]` count (Q5262).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Security] At the end of the battle, play this card without paying the cost` | `effects[0]` `trigger:"Security"`, `timing:"endOfBattle"` → `SubTrigger whenSecurityBattleEnded { once:true }` → `PlayWithoutCost { isSelfRef, isSelf, from:["trash"] }` | `waits for its security battle to end, plays itself and applies its On Play DP loss` — public attack, exact instance id in the battle area, `securityChecked` battle payload, and the `cardsMoved` event index proven to come **after** the battle; `still plays itself at the end of a security battle it lost` (new) — a 10000 DP attacker deletes the security Digimon and it is still played from the trash with its [On Play] attached |
| `[On Play] ... -3000 DP for the turn` | `effects[1]` `ModifyDP -3000 forTheTurn`, `target {controller:"opponent", kind:["Digimon"], count:1}` | `publicly applies mandatory On Play -3000 to exactly one opposing Digimon for this turn` — own board untouched, exactly one of two opponents affected (the 3000 DP one is deleted, the 4000 DP bystander is unchanged); `reduces exactly 4000 DP to 1000 and expires on the actual current-turn boundary` — real turn loop, 4000→1000, back to 4000 in the opponent's Main |
| `[When Digivolving] ... -3000 DP` | `effects[2]` | `publicly digivolves from a legal yellow level 3, draws, and applies the DP tail` (BT1-047 → BT23-028, memory 2→0, bonus draw, 7000→4000) |
| `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 2` / `linkDp` | `linkRequirement [{ cost:2, traits:["Appmon"] }]` | `links for 2, contributes 3000 DP and restricts an opposing Digimon when linking` (memory 5→3, host +3000, `linked` holds the exact instance); `rejects Link onto a non-Appmon without spending memory or moving Coordemon` (`link-requirement-unmet`) |
| `[When Linking] ... 1 of their Digimon can't activate [When Digivolving] effects` | `effects[3]` `isLinked` → `SubTrigger whenLinked {isSelfRef}` → `Restrict cannotActivateWhenDigivolving`, `duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | `publicly suppresses an opponent When Digivolving effect after linking` — real turn loop, the opponent's evolution into another Coordemon produces no DP change |
| Q5258 / Q5260 (no other effect may activate it) | same restriction | `linked suppression / unsuppressed control: external Diaboromon reactivation follows the When Digivolving restriction` — EX6-043's external re-activation makes a token in the control case and none in the restricted case |
| Q5259 / Q5262 (same effect still fires at [When Attacking]; no OPT consumed) | same restriction | `suppresses When Digivolving without consuming the later shared When Attacking link` — the opponent's BT23-021 is blocked at digivolution and the *same* shared clause still links at its next attack |
| Q5261 ("by" condition alone) | same restriction | `restricted / control: restricted TigerVespamon cannot process its mandatory by-placement` — the placement cost is not paid, no security card is added and no own Digimon is bounced in the restricted case |
| `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | `duration` | `expires the linked When Digivolving restriction at opponent turn end` — restriction alive through the whole opponent turn, gone when the next own Main opens |
| catalog fidelity | `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` | `matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause` |

| `When any of your cards with [Beast]/[Beastkin]/[CS] are played` | `effects[1]` `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger whenPlayed { controller:"mine", nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Beast","Beastkin","CS"], match:"trait"}] }` | `reacts to an own played %s and restricts exactly one opposing Digimon` over a mixed pool (AD1-010, BT1-049, BT1-031 Beast; BT1-035 Beastkin; BT23-017 CS Digimon; BT23-078 CS Tamer), with a bystander proved unaffected; `ignores a played card with no Beast, Beastkin, or CS trait`; `does not treat a near Appmon-only card as Beast, Beastkin, or CS` |

### BT23-030 — Etemon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog (`BT23-030`): Yellow/Black Lv.5 Ultimate, play cost 7, DP 7000, traits `Puppet`, `CS`.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[Sukamon] in name or w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`.
2. `＜Alliance＞`.
3. `[Main] [Once Per Turn] By paying 1 cost, you may play 1 play cost 3 or lower card with [Chuumon] or [Sukamon] in its name or the [CS] trait from your hand without paying the cost. Then, 1 of your level 3 or higher Digimon gains ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞ until your opponent's turn ends.`
4. Inherited: `＜Alliance＞`.

KB: **Q5273** — activation of the [Main] effect may **not** be declared while refusing the "by" condition. **Q5274** — without that payment the "then" tail cannot grant the keywords. **Q5275** — the playable pool is exactly "play cost 3 or lower with [Chuumon]/[Sukamon] in its name, or play cost 3 or lower with the [CS] trait".

Note on the printed name gate: `w/[Sukamon] in name` and `with [Chuumon] or [Sukamon] in its name` are substring gates, so the module correctly uses `names` / `match: "name"` rather than the exact forms.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[Sukamon] in name: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement[0] { level:4, names:["Sukamon"], cost:3 }` | covered by the evolution `it.each` block (named and CS routes with source-stack identity, cost and bonus draw) |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement[1] { level:4, traits:["CS"], cost:3 }` | same block |
| `＜Alliance＞` printed | `effects[0].keywords` | `publicly accepts printed Alliance and suspends the chosen ally` / `publicly refuses printed Alliance and leaves the ally unsuspended` (real attacks, security count as the receipt) |
| `＜Alliance＞` inherited | `effects[3]` `isInherited` | the same two `it.each` cases with BT23-030 as a digivolution card under BT1-016 |
| `[Main]` activation | `effects[1]` `trigger:"Main"` | `activates Main through the advertised public effect and pays exactly 1` — the effect key is read from `activatableEffectsJson` and driven by a public `activateEffect` intent |
| `By paying 1 cost` (mandatory once declared, Q5273) | `CostGatedBlock { cost: payMemory 1 }` with **no** `optional` / `abortOnDecline` | `cannot decline the By payment once activation is declared (Q5273)` — with `autoDeclineOptional` on, memory still moves 5→4 and both keywords still land; `may decline only the play, still pays 1 and grants both mandatory keywords` asserts exactly **one** optional decision is ever raised, and it is the play |
| affordability gate | `canPayCost` on `payMemory` | `is not offered when its 1 memory cost is unaffordable` — at `memory = MEMORY_MIN (-10)` the effect is either not advertised or the intent is refused; nothing is paid and no keyword is granted |
| `you may play 1 ... from your hand without paying the cost` | inner `PlayWithoutCost { from:["hand"], payCost:false, optional:true, target.upTo:true }` | `pays exactly 1, plays only an eligible card free and grants both keywords to one Digimon` (public `activateEffect`); `plays a cost-3 Chuumon-name card for free and grants both keywords to one Digimon`; `free-plays a cost-3 CS Tamer as a word-card eligible target` |
| play-cost and name/trait boundaries (Q5275) | `playCostLte: 3`, `nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Chuumon","Sukamon"], match:"name"}, {tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}]` | `does not free-play an opponent card or a cost-4/near-trait card`; `refuses a cost-4 name match and a near-trait card, yet still pays 1 and grants the keywords`; `free-plays PlatinumSukamon because [Sukamon] in name is a substring gate` |
| zone gate ("from your hand") | `from: ["hand"]` | `plays only from the hand, never from the trash or deck` (new) — identical eligible copies sitting in trash and deck are untouched, the cost is still paid and the tail still lands |
| `Then, 1 of your level 3 or higher Digimon gains ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞` (Q5274) | two `GainKeyword` actions, `levelComparison { op:"gte", value:3 }`, second with `sameTarget: true`, both mandatory | `gives the same level 3-or-higher Digimon both Reboot and Blocker` (IR); `never grants the keywords to the non-Digimon CS card it just played`; every public activation test asserts exactly one permanent holds both keywords |
| `until your opponent's turn ends` + combat behaviour | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | `granted Reboot unsuspends the target in the opponent's turn and granted Blocker blocks` |
| `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | `rejects a same-source Main reactivation in the same turn`; `resets the once per turn limit for the same physical Etemon on its controller's next turn` (real turn loop) |

#### Corrections
**Module `BT23-030.ts` (behaviour change).** The `[Main]` clause's `CostGatedBlock` carried `optional: true, abortOnDecline: true`, which made the engine ask "Pay cost: 1 memory?" and, on a refusal, abandon the whole clause. That let a player declare the activation and then decline the printed "by" condition — exactly what Q5273 and CR 15-8-4-4-1 forbid. Both flags are removed, so the payment is now mandatory once the public `activateEffect` is declared and affordability alone gates whether the effect can be activated. The inner `PlayWithoutCost` keeps `optional: true` (the printed "you may play"), and the two `GainKeyword` actions stay mandatory on one shared target.

Test changes:
- Rewrote the three tests that encoded the old contract: the IR assertion now asserts the two flags are **absent**; the "decline the play" test now uses a public `activateEffect` and asserts only one optional prompt is raised; the old "may decline the By payment" test is replaced by `cannot decline the By payment once activation is declared (Q5273)`.
- Added `is not offered when its 1 memory cost is unaffordable` and `plays only from the hand, never from the trash or deck`.
- Replaced the last injected `advance().fire(EffectTiming.OnDeclaration, ...)` with a public `activateEffect` and removed the now-unused `EffectTiming` import.
- Replaced the two `as any` casts on compiled-IR reads with non-null assertions.

→ `Test Files  1 passed (1)` / `Tests  27 passed (27)`.

| Then, 3 or fewer → `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` | `SecurityManipulation op:"addTop", source:"deck"` gated by `zoneCount security lte 3` | boundaries at 4 pre-removal (recovers, deck top identity asserted), 5 pre-removal (no recovery), 0 pre-removal (Q5276) |
| Inherited `＜Alliance＞` | `Static` effect, `isInherited`, `keywords:[Alliance]` | public combat: `suspends the accepted ally, adds its DP and raises the security attack count`; refusal path; two security checks when the boosted carrier attacks the player |

### BT23-032 — Shakkoumon

#### Contract and rules evidence

Catalog: Yellow/Black Digimon, Lv.5, play cost 8, DP 8000, evoCosts Yellow Lv.4 / Black Lv.4 cost 4,
types Mutant + Hudie + CS + Angel. Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`
2. `[DNA Digivolve] Yellow Lv.4 + black/blue Lv.4: Cost 0`
3. `[When Digivolving]` grant one opponent Digimon "[Start of Your Main Phase] This Digimon attacks."
   until their turn ends; then, if DNA digivolving, `＜De-Digivolve 1＞` one opponent Digimon.
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` leave-the-battle-area replacement, other than by your effects:
   may play 1 level 4 or lower yellow, black **or** [CS] Digimon from its digivolution cards free.
5. Same text as an inherited effect.

KB: Q5277 (grant lands on an unaffectable Digimon but never triggers), Q5278/Q5279 (the pool is
"yellow or black" **OR** "[CS] trait" — an OR, not an AND), Q6250 (Barrier first, then the source
play; playing BT23-027 Angemon out of the stack loses Barrier). Banlist: restricted to 1.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| CS alternate cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:4, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | `publicly evolves from a level-4 [CS] source for 3 even when its colour is off-recipe` (green BT22-047); negatives for a Lv.3 CS source and a Lv.4 non-CS source |
| Printed Yellow/Black Lv.4 cost 4 | catalog `evoCosts` | `publicly normal-evolves from a level-4 yellow/black source for 4 and draws the evolution bonus` — stack identity, memory 4→0, bonus draw |
| Both DNA recipes at cost 0 | `dnaDigivolveRequirement` with Yellow+Black and Yellow+Blue | `publicly DNA digivolves for 0 with a yellow level 4 plus a black/blue level 4` through BT23-027 Angemon's `[On Play]` (the only public DNA route): both materials end in the stack, memory drops only by Angemon's play cost, both draws land; negative with a Lv.3 material |
| `[When Digivolving]` forced-attack grant | `GrantAuraToOpponents` + `duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | `makes the chosen opponent Digimon attack at the start of their main phase, and only that turn` — a real seat-1 turn through `advance().runTurn(1)`; one `attackDeclared` at the player, grant gone after `turnEnded` |
| Then, if DNA digivolving, `＜De-Digivolve 1＞` | `DeDigivolve` gated by `condition {kind:"isDnaDigivolving"}` | positive via the Angemon DNA route (victim's top card in trash, base survives); negative `does not De-Digivolve when it digivolves normally` |
| Leave-area replacement, other than by your effects | `Replacement wouldLeavePlay`, `leaveCause:"otherThanYourEffect"`, `PlayWithoutCost from digivolutionCards`, `optional`, `orFilters` yellow/black OR [CS] | **converted to a public flow**: the opponent plays BT15-089 Meteor Wing to delete it (yellow Lv.4 source and a purple [CS]-only Lv.4 source, proving Q5278's OR); plus a public battle deletion; negative for an own-effect removal; decline path; empty-pool path |
| Q5279 inherited copy | second `AllTurns` effect, `isInherited` | `Q5279 applies the same replacement from a stack where Shakkoumon is a digivolution card` |
| Q6250 | — | `Q6250 plays a source card after ＜Barrier＞ prevents the battle deletion` (public combat, Barrier accepted, Angemon leaves the stack for the battle area) |

#### Corrections

- `BT23-032.test.ts`: **every `s.state.turnSeat` write is gone** (8 sites). All of them now reach the
  opponent's turn through `startTurnLoop` plus public `endPhase`/`endMainPhaseIfOpen`:
  - the forced-attack grant test and the Q5277 test drive seat 1's whole turn through the loop
    instead of `advance().runTurn(1)` with a hand-set seat and memory;
  - the four opponent-removal tests (battle deletion, decline path, empty-pool path, Q5279) hand the
    turn over publicly; the three that needed an opponent **effect** now have seat 1 play BT15-089
    Meteor Wing rather than `advance().verb.deletePermanent`;
  - the two battle tests (battle deletion, Q6250) make Shakkoumon attack on its own turn so it is
    suspended and legally attackable when the opponent's turn arrives — the production loop
    unsuspends board-laid permanents at the seat's Active phase, so `suspended: true` in the Board
    Spec does not survive a real turn;
  - the own-effect negative simply drops its `turnSeat = 0` write (a no-op: the harness starts there).
- `BT23-032.test.ts`: opponent-effect deletion converted to the public BT15-089 Meteor Wing route
  (new fixture constant `OPPONENT_DELETE_OPTION`). Seat 0 keeps exactly one security card so the
  option's "minus 2000 per security card" cap stays above every target, and an empty stack cannot
  end the game mid-test.
- `BT23-032.test.ts`: removed a stale "Retained red" comment on the Q6250 test — that test passes;
  the described Barrier seam no longer exists.

```

```
oxlint: clean. oxfmt --check: clean. `tsc --noEmit` (apps/api): no errors.

#### Retained reds and gaps

- No `it.fails`. The coordinator's known seam (effect-driven DNA ignoring "can't digivolve" in
  `engine/effects/interpreter/actions/dna.ts`) was not reached by any test here.
- Gap: the `[Once Per Turn]` cap on the leave replacement has no behavioural proof. The only way to
  measure a second removal in the same turn is to survive the first one, and the only survival route
  is ＜Barrier＞ from BT23-027 Angemon in the stack. I built that test and abandoned it: with two
  Barrier-prevented attacks in one turn the engine still deleted the host on the first exchange
  (observed: `barrierResolved`, security 2→1, yet Shakkoumon reached the trash), which is a separate
  question from this card's once-per-turn key and is not safe for me to chase inside a card lane.
  Recommend the engine lane reproduce: BT23-032 with `under: [BT23-027, BT1-051]`, suspended,
  attacked by an 11000 DP attacker, Barrier accepted. Structural coverage of `frequency:
  "OncePerTurn"` remains in `models both leave-play replacements as a once-per-turn optional
  own-stack play`.
- One `advance().verb.deletePermanent` remains, in the own-effect negative: the clause needs a
  deletion *by the controller's own effect*, and no catalog card in a yellow/black shell deletes the
  controller's own Lv.5 Digimon cleanly. Named in the test.
- Side note from the conversions, useful to other lanes: `startTurnLoop` runs the seat's Active
  phase, so a Board Spec `suspended: true` on the seat that moves first is undone before its Main
  opens. Suspend through a real attack instead.

### BT23-033 — Beautymon

#### Contract and rules evidence

Catalog: Yellow/Black Digimon, Lv.5, play cost 8, DP 8000, forms Ult. + Appmon, type Beauty,
`linkDp 4000`, `linkRequirement "[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 3"`,
`linkEffect "[When Linking] Until your opponent's turn ends, their effects can't return this Digimon
to hands or decks or affect it with ＜De-Digivolve＞ effects."`

Printed clauses:
1. `[App Fusion] [Coordemon] & [Consulmon]: Cost 0`
2. `＜Barrier＞`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` you may link 1 level 4 or lower Digimon card from your trash or
   this Digimon's digivolution cards to this Digimon without paying the cost.
4. `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn]` when this Digimon gets linked, if you have 5 or fewer security
   cards, `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` Then, give 1 opponent Digimon -1000 DP until their turn ends for
   each of your security cards.
5. The `[Link]` requirement and the `[When Linking]` link effect.

KB: Q5280 (cannot link a card that has no `＜Link＞`), Q5281 (the "then" half runs even when the
Recovery "if" fails).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| App Fusion cost 0 | `appFusionRequirement [{names:["Coordemon","Consulmon"], cost:0}]` | public `appFusion` intent: `App Fusions from a linked [Coordemon] + [Consulmon] pair for 0 and refuses a wrong partner` — Consulmon is linked to Coordemon for the printed `[Link]` cost 2, the fusion itself costs 0 (memory unchanged at 3), both names end up in the stack, `linked` empties, the evolution bonus draw lands, and a `digivolved / mechanic: "appFusion"` event is emitted; negative: the same fusion with BT23-039 as the linked partner is refused and nothing moves. `appFusionCostFor` in both name orders is still asserted structurally |
| `＜Barrier＞` | `Static` keyword | public combat pair `public Barrier combat accepts/refuses the deletion` — accepted: survives, one security card trashed; refused: deleted |
| `[On Play]` may link ≤ Lv.4 from trash or own stack | `Link` action, `hasLinkRequirement: true`, `levelComparison lte 4`, `from:["trash","digivolutionCards"]`, `optional` | **converted to a public flow**: `links only a Link-capable level-4-or-lower card...` now plays Beautymon from hand for 8 (memory 10→2); the Link-capable BT23-039 is linked, the non-Link BT1-009 stays in trash (Q5280) |
| `[When Digivolving]` half | separate `WhenDigivolving` effect with the same action | `when digivolving links from this Digimon's stack, not another friendly stack` — the other host's stack is untouched |
| `[Your Turn][Once Per Turn]` on getting linked → Recovery gate | `SubTrigger whenLinked` + `SecurityManipulation addTop/deck` gated by `zoneCount security lte 5` | `at five security recovers first, then scales the DP loss from all six cards, per Q5281` (now a public play); `places the exact deck top card onto the top of the security stack`; at six security no recovery |
| Then -1000 DP per own security card | `ModifyDP -1000` with `scaling {per:1, unit:"security", filter:{controller:"mine"}}` | 6 security → 10000 DP target reads 4000 in several tests; runs even when Recovery is skipped |
| Once per turn / reset | `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` | `resets the once-per-turn link effect on the next own turn through the real turn loop` (turn loop, second link on the next own turn); `publicly triggers its linked Your Turn effect once, with security scaling and a second-link cap` |
| `[When Linking]` protections | `AllTurns` `isLinked` effect: `Restrict cannotReturnToHandOrDeck` + `GrantStatic protection beDeDigivolved` | `links onto an Appmon for 3, adds 4000 DP, and grants both linked protections` — cost 3 paid, host DP +4000, both restrictions live |

KB (9 Q&A, all covered):
Q5282 (what the restriction blocks), Q5283 (a shared `[When Digivolving] [When Attacking]` effect
still activates on attack), Q5284 (an external "activate that card's `[When Digivolving]`" cannot
either), Q5285 (a suppressed effect does not process its "by" cost), Q5286 (the suppressed timing
does not consume the `[X Per Turn]` use), Q5289–Q5292 (face-up security cards: they stay revealed,
check normally, still trigger `[Security]`, and are re-hidden by a shuffle).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| CS alternate cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | `accepts off-color level-5 CS evolution and rejects an off-color non-CS peer` |
| Cost -5 with a [Zaxon] Tamer | Static `Replacement wouldBePlayed` → `reduceCost 5` gated by `youHave {kind:["Tamer"], trait "Zaxon"}` | `pays 5 less with a Zaxon Tamer...` (memory 10→4) and `does not reduce the play cost without a Zaxon Tamer` (10→-1) |
| Restriction + -6000 across three timings, shared once per turn | three effects (`OnPlay`, `WhenDigivolving`, `WhenAttacking`) with `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` and a common `sharedUseKey`; `Restrict cannotActivateWhenDigivolving` + `ModifyDP -6000` with `sameTarget:true`, both `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | new `spends its own shared once-per-turn use on the first timing and re-arms next own turn`: digivolve applies -6000, an attack in the same turn does not re-apply, the opponent's turn ends the duration, and the next own turn's attack applies it again; `expires the restriction and the -6000 at opponent turn end` |
| Q5282/Q5285/Q5286 | as above | `suppresses the opponent's When Digivolving half but leaves the shared When Attacking use` — the opponent's ST24-07 cannot fire on digivolve, then fires on attack; `does not process the By cost of a suppressed effect` as a restricted/unrestricted pair |
| Q5284 | as above | `blocks an external activation of a restricted When Digivolving effect` as a restricted/unrestricted pair against EX6-043 |
| `[On Deletion]` face-up bottom security | `SecurityManipulation op:"placeAsSecurity", toTop:false, faceUp:true` | Q5289 `places the deleted card face up behind the existing security card` (public combat, real turn loop, face flags per position); Q5290 `checks the face-up placed card as a normal security card`; Q5291 `triggers the Security effect of a face-up security card`; Q5292 `re-hides every face-up security card on a shuffle` |

### BT23-035 — Dynasmon

#### Contract and rules evidence

Catalog: Yellow/Red Digimon, Lv.6, play cost 12, DP 12000, evoCosts Yellow Lv.5 / Red Lv.5 cost 4,
types Holy Warrior + Royal Knight + CS. Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[Witchelny]/[CS] trait: Cost 3`
2. `＜Barrier＞`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` by trashing your top security card, all of your opponent's Digimon
   get -6000 DP for the turn.
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` when your security stack is removed from, this Digimon gains
   `＜Security A. +1＞` until your turn ends. Then, if you have 3 or fewer security cards,
   `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞`.

KB: Q5293 (a later-played opposing Digimon at 0 DP is deleted by the rule check before its
`[On Play]` can activate), Q5294 (a `[Security]` effect resolves ahead of "when a card is removed
from the security stack" watchers), Q5295 (the per-turn cap re-arms on the next turn), Q5296
(＜Barrier＞ trashing your own security card fires this trigger and the extra check happens).

Rules check on the "by" cost: comprehensive 15-7-1 and 15-7-4 make "By trashing your top security
card, ..." an **optional** processing condition on a trigger-type effect, so the module's
`optional: true` + `abortOnDecline: true` is correct (15-8-4-4-1 governs activation-type effects,
which this is not).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Witchelny/CS alternate cost 3 | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:5, traits:["Witchelny","CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | `evolves publicly from a Witchelny Lv.5 for the alternate cost of 3`; printed Yellow and Red Lv.5 routes at 4 with the bonus draw; negative for a Lv.5 that is neither |
| `＜Barrier＞` | `Static` keyword | `exposes Barrier through the live keyword seam`, and the public Q5296 combat below |
| `[On Play][When Digivolving]` by trashing top security → all opposing Digimon -6000 for the turn | `ModifyDP playerWide`, `count:"all"`, `duration:"forTheTurn"`, `cost {kind:"trash", zone:"security", position:"top"}`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | `reduces current and later-played opposing Digimon for the turn`; `applies the same -6000 DP through a public evolution and restores it at the real turn end`; `does not reduce DP when its security-trash cost cannot be paid`; `may decline the top-security processing condition before trashing or reducing DP`; Q5293 `deletes a later-played 0-DP opposing Digimon before its On Play effect can activate` |
| `[All Turns][Once Per Turn]` on own-security removal → `＜Security A. +1＞` until your turn ends | `SubTrigger whenSecurityRemoved` with `sourceFilter {controller:"mine"}` + `GainKeyword SecurityAttack 1`, `untilYourTurnEnd` | public: Q5294 `resolves the checked Security effect before its own security-removal trigger` (real attack and security check, event ordering asserted); **converted to public**: `skips Recovery while more than 3 security cards remain but still grants Security Attack +1`; Q5296 `checks an extra security card after Barrier trashes its own security` |
| Then, 3 or fewer → `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` | `SecurityManipulation addTop/deck` gated by `zoneCount security lte 3` | deck TOP identity asserted (not a deck-wide selection); opponent-stack removal ignored; Q5295 cap and next-own-turn re-arm |

   Digimon may attack.

### BT23-037 — Tentomon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog: Green/Yellow Lv.3 Rookie, play cost 3, DP 1000, traits Insectoid/Hudie/CS.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[CS] trait: Cost 0`.
2. `[Your Turn]` When this Digimon would digivolve into a Digimon card with the [CS] trait, reduce
   the digivolution cost by 1.
3. Inherited `[When Attacking] [Once Per Turn]` You may play 1 play cost 5 or lower Digimon card
   with the [Hudie] trait from your hand without paying the cost. The Digimon this effect played
   can't digivolve and is deleted at the end of your opponent's turn.
KB (`query.mjs card BT23-037`): Q5299 (does not trigger in the breeding area), Q5300 (the played
Digimon may be the ＜Alliance＞ ally of the same attack), Q5565 (it IS deleted at the deletion
timing; if a prevention saves it, the can't-digivolve prohibition stays), Q5566 (the delayed
deletion and an end-of-turn trigger are simultaneous, so the turn player orders them). All four have
behavioural tests.
The catalog `effectText` contains U+00A0 before "trait"; the test pins the printed text through a
local `printedText()` normaliser and does not touch the catalog.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Digivolve Lv.2 w/[CS]: Cost 0 | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:2,traits:["CS"],cost:0,isAlternate:true}]` | "digivolves for 0 over a level-2 CS card and rejects a level-2 non-CS card" (public digivolve from breeding, memory unchanged at 3, stack identity, bonus draw; negative returns `invalid-evolution`) |
| [Your Turn] CS digivolution cost −1 (Q5299) | `YourTurn` → `Replacement wouldDigivolve`, `sourceFilter {isSelfRef,zone:"battleArea"}`, `into nameOrTrait [{CS,trait}]`, inner `reduceCost 1` | "reduces the CS digivolution cost by 1 only from the battle area" (7 rows): battle area vs breeding, ordinary and alternate routes, alternate-only off-colour target, and a same-colour non-CS target; each row pays the exact memory through a public `digivolve` and asserts stack, bonus draw and `assertNoLoudGap` |
| [Your Turn] restriction | same `YourTurn` effect | "offers no CS reduction on the opponent's turn" — **rewritten** to move the turn owner with `startTurnLoop` and assert the reduction is 1 / 0 / 1 across seat 0, seat 1 and seat 0 again |
| Inherited play of a Lv≤5 [Hudie] Digimon from hand, free | `WhenAttacking isInherited frequency OncePerTurn` → `PlayWithoutCost` (`playCostLte:5`, `Hudie` trait, `optional`, `abortOnDecline`, `bindResultAs:"playedHudie"`) | "plays only a hand Hudie Digimon of play cost 5 or lower when the host attacks": a public attack from a real BT23-050-over-BT23-037 stack; the too-expensive card, the non-Hudie card, the Hudie **Tamer** and the Hudie in the **trash** all stay put. Decline path proved separately |
| "can't digivolve", bound to that instance | `Restrict digivolve duration permanent` targeting `boundRef:"playedHudie"` | "permanently blocks digivolution of the played Digimon while an untouched twin still digivolves" — same card id, same route, a copy this effect did not play still digivolves |
| "deleted at the end of your opponent's turn" (Q5565) | `DelayedDelete timing:"endOfOpponentTurn"` | "deletes the played Digimon at the end of the opponent's turn, not the controller's" (survives the controller's end step, is trashed at the opponent's); "keeps the evolution prohibition when the deletion is prevented" (BT23-073 Eater Bit pays with itself; the survivor is still restricted) |
| Q5300 ＜Alliance＞ interaction | — (peer card BT23-041 grants Alliance) | "offers the newly played Digimon to a pending Alliance…" (the played permanent appears in `eligibleAllyIds`, suspends when chosen) and "adds the played ally's DP to the attacking host" (9000 + 5000 read inside the battle) |
| Q5566 simultaneous end-of-turn processing | — | "resolves the deletion at the same end-of-turn boundary as the opponent's effect" (BT23-036's ＜Raid＞ attack still sees the Digimon, so security is untouched) and "offers the turn player the end-of-turn processing order" (`orderTriggers` decision for seat 1 with 2 keys) |
| Once per turn identity | `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` | "caps the inherited play at once per turn and resets it on the next own turn" (real turn loop) and "tracks the once-per-turn cap per Tentomon copy" (two hosts each play one) |

#### Corrections
`BT23-037.test.ts` only: the "offers no CS reduction on the opponent's turn" case wrote

and not the exact form, `"Hudiemon X"` does not match exact `Hudiemon`. Correct as printed.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Digivolve Lv.2 w/[CS]: Cost 0 | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:2,traits:["CS"],cost:0,isAlternate:true}]` | "digivolves for 0 from a level-2 CS Digi-Egg in breeding and rejects a non-CS egg": public `digivolve` with `useAlternateCost`, memory unchanged at 5; the non-CS egg returns `invalid-evolution` |
| [Start of Your Main Phase] … may digivolve into [Hudiemon] … cost reduced by 2 | `StartOfYourMainPhase` → `Digivolve` on self, `into nameExact Hudiemon`, `from ["hand","trash"]`, `payCost:true`, `reduceCost:2`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | Fires through the production turn loop after a fully public stack assembly (`raiseWormmonToOwnMain`): the hand Hudiemon case asserts the full stack `[Erika, egg, Wormmon]`, the new top card, memory 5 (+1 from Erika's own clause) − 3 = 3, and both draws; a second case takes the alternate [CS] route via `preferOptionIndex: 1` for 4; a third digivolves into a **trash** Hudiemon |

Negatives: Q5302 (Erika in hand and in trash → no activation offered at all, memory untouched), another [Hudie]/[CS] Tamer (BT23-081) and the **opponent's** Erika both refused |
| "may" / optional | `optional:true, abortOnDecline:true` | Decline leaves Erika and her under-card on the field, the stack at `[egg]`, the trash empty, and only Erika's own +1 memory moved. A separate case proves nothing is paid when no Hudiemon is available anywhere |

| Inherited [All Turns] all own [Hudie] +1000 | `AllTurns isInherited` → `ModifyDP` `controller:"mine"`, `nameOrTrait [{Hudie,trait}]`, `count:"all"` | Mixed pool: own carrier 8000, own Hudie peer 2000, own non-Hudie peer 3000, opponent's Hudie 1000. `BT23-040.test.ts` only (no module change):

   owner is now moved only by the production turn loop, and the clause is counted by the
   `effectTriggered` key `BT23-040/ir-1-0` across seat 0 → seat 1 → seat 0 (1 / 1 / 2).
2. The shared stack-assembly helper no longer writes `s.state.phase`. `raiseWormmon` +
   `openMainPhase` are replaced by one `raiseWormmonToOwnMain(s, { memory, evolveMemory })` that
   runs the production turn loop end to end:

     asserting the stack is `[BT23-002]` and the permanent is still `inBreeding`;

     public `moveFromBreeding` intent;

     already resolved.
   `closeTurn` now closes that loop with a public `surrender`.
3. Follow-on assertion update: the stack is now assembled across two of the controller's own turns,
   so the hand in the first case holds three draws (turn-1 digivolution bonus, turn-2 draw phase,

for this card (KB-INDEX lists "none"). Alliance is read against comprehensive rules §16-24-1.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 3, traits: ["CS"], cost: 2, isAlternate: true }]` | public `digivolve` intent — "digivolves from a Green/Yellow level-3 source on the normal route" (BT23-037, cost 3) and "an off-color level-3 [CS] source on the alternate route" (BT23-017, cost 2, `useAlternateCost`+`alternateRequirementIndex`); stack identity, memory and the bonus draw asserted.
