| `＜Alliance＞` | `Static` effect with `keywords: [{ keyword: "Alliance" }]` | "exposes Alliance on the live board" (`observe().hasKeyword`) plus "suspends an ally for Alliance without re-triggering its own suspension clause" — public `attack` + `respondAlliance` with the ally named, ending on the extra security check (3 → 1). Public flow. |
| `[All Turns][OPT] when this suspends, 1 of your Digimon gains ＜Piercing＞ and +3000 DP for the turn` | `AllTurns` effect, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`, `SubTrigger{event:"whenSuspended", sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}}` → `GainKeyword(Piercing, forTheTurn)` + `ModifyDP(+3000, forTheTurn, sameTarget:true)` | Positive: "suspends by attacking, then the chosen Digimon wins its battle at +3000 DP and pierces for a security check" — both grants proved at their endpoints (a 4000 defender dies only with the +3000, and Piercing forces a second security check). Opponent-driven suspension: "also triggers when an opponent's effect suspends it during the opponent's turn" (BT1-110 played publicly by seat 1 through the real turn loop). #### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog: Green Digimon, Lv.5, play cost 7, DP 7000, evoCost Green Lv.4 cost 3, form Ultimate,
attribute Data, types `["Fairy","CS"]`.
Printed clauses:

2. `When this card would be played from the hand, if you have [Yuuko Kamishiro] or a [CS] trait Digimon, reduce the play cost by 3.`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By suspending 1 Digimon, until your opponent's turn ends, their effects can't return 1 of your Digimon with [Vegetation], [Plant] or [Fairy] in any of its traits or the [CS] trait to hands or decks.`
4. Inherited: `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon deletes your opponent's Digimon in battle, trash their top security card.`

KB: **Q5305** (the suspend cost may take either player's Digimon) and **Q5306** (the inherited
effect does not activate when this Digimon and the opponent's are deleted together). Both covered.
No errata.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 4, traits:["CS"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]` | "digivolves from an off-color CS level 4 for 3 on the alternate route, with or without the flag" (BT22-022), stack identity and memory asserted; "digivolves for 3 on the Green route, draws 1 …" proves the printed Green route and the bonus draw; negative "rejects a level 3 non-CS source" → `{ok:false, reason:"invalid-evolution"}`. Public flow. |
| Play-cost reduction by 3 | `Static` → `Replacement{event:"wouldBePlayed", sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}}` wrapping `Replacement{mode:"reduceCost", amount:3, condition: youHave(zone:"battleArea", or:[Tamer nameExact "Yuuko Kamishiro", Digimon trait "CS"])}` | "charges 4 memory with Yuuko Kamishiro / a CS Digimon in the battle area, otherwise 7" — three-way `it.each` through the public `playCard` intent with memory endpoints 6/6/3. Rules boundary: "ignores a CS Digimon in the breeding area when checking the cost-reduction condition" (comprehensive rules 3-4-5-8). `[Yuuko Kamishiro]` is a printed bracket name, so `nameExact`, not substring — pinned in the IR-shape test. Public flow. |
| `[On Play][When Digivolving]` suspend cost + return restriction | `OnPlay` and `WhenDigivolving` each → `Restrict{restriction:"cannotReturnToHandOrDeck", byOpponentEffectsOnly:true, duration:"untilOpponentTurnEnd", cost: suspend(1 Digimon, controller:"any"), optional:true, abortOnDecline:true}`, target `kind:["Digimon"], controller:"mine", or:[traitContains ["Vegetation","Plant","Fairy"], traits ["CS"]], count:1` | Q5305: "pays the entry cost by suspending an opponent's Digimon and offers no Tamer as a candidate" — the cost candidate set spans both seats; the protection candidate set excludes the [CS] Tamer Yuuko because the text says "1 of your **Digimon**". Trait modes (new): "protects a Digimon whose trait merely contains Plant, but not one whose trait merely contains CS" — BT1-071 Vegiemon ([Carnivorous Plant]) qualifies, BT16-039 Pulsemon ([Abadin Electronics], which contains "cs") does not. Positive endpoint: "digivolves for 3 on the Green route … and protects itself from an opponent's return effect" — the opponent's BT13-105 is played publicly on their real turn and cannot move it; the restriction then lapses at that turn's end. Negatives: declined entry effect leaves nothing suspended or protected, and the same opponent bounce then succeeds. Controller scope: "still lets its own controller return the protected Digimon with their own effect" (`byOpponentEffectsOnly`). Public flow. |
| Inherited `[All Turns][OPT]` security trash on battle deletion | `AllTurns`, `isInherited:true`, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`, `SubTrigger{event:"whenDeletesInBattle", sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}}` → `SecurityManipulation{op:"trashTop", controller:"opponent", amount:1}` | Positive: "trashes the opponent's top security after its carrier deletes a Digimon in battle" (BT23-044 as a digivolution card under BT1-080). Q5306: "does not trash security when both battling Digimon are deleted". Once-per-turn: "trashes security once per turn and again on the next turn" — the host suspends by attacking on its own turn, the opponent's two attacks in one turn trash exactly one security card, and the gate resets on the following real turn. Scope: "does not trash security after its carrier wins against a Security Digimon" (a Security Digimon is not "your opponent's Digimon" on the battle area). Public flow. |

#### Corrections
- **Module bug fixed** (`BT23-044.ts`): the Restrict target used
  `or: [{trait:"Vegetation"},{trait:"Plant"},{trait:"Fairy"},{trait:"CS"}]`. A bare `trait:` is an
  EXACT type match (`engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:80`), so "in any of its
  traits" was being treated as exact. Replaced with
  `or: [{ traitContains: ["Vegetation","Plant","Fairy"] }, { traits: ["CS"] }]` — substring for the
  three plain traits, exact for the bracket trait `[CS]`. Real catalog cases this changes:
  `Carnivorous Plant`, `Ancient Plant`, `Ancient Fairy` now qualify; `Abadin Electronics`
  (which contains "cs") still correctly does not. Header comment updated to record the reasoning.
  Reverting the fix fails two focused assertions, verified.
- `BT23-044.test.ts`: added "protects a Digimon whose trait merely contains Plant, but not one whose
  trait merely contains CS" and tightened the IR-shape assertion to the new match modes.
- `BT23-044.test.ts`: removed all four `s.state.turnSeat = …` writes. The two digivolve/protection
  tests and the once-per-turn test now run on a single `s.engine.startTurnLoop()`, stepping through
  seat 0's Main → seat 1's Main → seat 0's Main with `advance().waitForMainPhase` /
  `endMainPhaseIfOpen`. The once-per-turn fixture no longer seeds `suspended: true` (which the
  Active phase would clear); the host suspends by publicly attacking on its own turn, and the
  security accounting was restated accordingly.

area" uses `advance(engine).verb.deletePermanent(..., "byEffect")`. Overflow is an engine-wide ACE
rule rather than a clause of this card's effect body, and the only public route (losing a battle to
a >12000 DP inert Digimon on the opponent's turn) would make the memory endpoint racy, because a
memory swing past zero ends the turn mid-assertion. Every printed clause of the card itself is
proved through public intents.

#### Catalog discrepancies
None. No non-breaking space in this entry; `isAce`/`overflowMemory` and all text match the printed
card.

KB: Q5317, Q5318, Q5319 — all three covered.

- Q5318 (a Digimon played by BT23-017 Betamon's inherited effect cannot be a DNA material, because it can't digivolve): "refuses the DNA after Betamon's inherited attack play restricts digivolution", with a control test.
- Q5319 (the DNA result arrives unsuspended and can be an ＜Alliance＞ ally): "leaves the DNA result unsuspended and eligible as an Alliance ally".

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| `[Digivolve] [Armadillomon]/Lv.3 w/[CS]: Cost 2` | two alternates: `{ namesExact: ["Armadillomon"], cost: 2 }` and `{ level: 3, traits: ["CS"], cost: 2 }` | "refuses an ineligible level-3 source on both the alternate and the printed routes" (public negatives) and "gates the printed route on the exact base name, not a substring" — `namesExact`, so a card merely containing "Armadillomon" does not qualify |
| `＜Blocker＞` (printed and inherited) | two `Static` keyword effects, the second `isInherited: true` | "exposes Blocker directly and from a realistic inherited stack" — live keyword read on both the card itself and a host that only inherits it |
| `1 of your opponent's Digimon gets -2000 DP until their turn ends` | `ModifyDP` `controller: opponent`, `count: 1`, `amount: -2000`, `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | "publicly plays for 5, reduces exactly one opposing Digimon and may decline the DNA" and "keeps the -2000 through the opponent's turn and restores it on the next own turn" (real turn loop across the duration boundary) |
| `Then, if it's your turn, 2 of your Digimon may DNA digivolve into [Shakkoumon] in the hand` | `DnaDigivolve`, `materials: { controller: mine, kind: Digimon, count: 2 }`, `into: { nameExact "Shakkoumon", zone: "hand" }`, `payCost: true`, `condition: { kind: "isYourTurn" }`, `optional: true` | "publicly DNA digivolves two of your Digimon into an unsuspended Shakkoumon for no extra memory"; the "if it's your turn" gate by "applies the DP reduction but skips the DNA clause when played on the opponent's turn"; the optional branch by the decline case; Q5317, Q5318, Q5319 as above |

#### Q5318, reproduced precisely (the coordinator's open question)
Fixture: a blue Lv.4 Garurumon (BT23-018) with BT23-017 Betamon in its digivolution cards attacks; Betamon's inherited `[When Attacking]` plays BT23-050 Ankylomon from hand for free, which makes that Ankylomon unable to digivolve. Ankylomon's own `[On Play]` then derives, and the host plus the just-played Ankylomon are the only pair that could satisfy Shakkoumon's `[DNA Digivolve]`.

What actually happens: **the engine refuses the DNA, which is the correct answer.** `observe().isRestricted(played, "digivolve")` is true, Shakkoumon stays in hand, the board still holds exactly two permanents, the host still has BT23-018 on top and there is no pending decision. So the reading recorded by the BT23-027 lane (Q5256 refusal works) holds here too; the red the BT23-017 lane retained — "effect-driven DNA ignores can't digivolve" — does **not** reproduce on this path. I kept no `it.fails`: the engine behaves correctly, and inventing a red would be wrong.

Shakkoumon now suspends itself by attacking on seat 0's own turn, the real turn loop hands the turn over, and the opponent's attack (which triggers Shakkoumon's would-leave replacement and replays Ankylomon) happens genuinely on the opponent's turn. Both seats got security and deck depth to survive a real turn, and the test ends with a surrender awaiting the loop.
- Added exact `effectText` / `inheritedEffectText` assertions with U+00A0 normalised in the test.

Printed clauses:

2. `[Your Turn] When any of your Option cards are placed in the battle area, this Digimon may digivolve into a Digimon card with [Cyberdramon] in its name or the [CS] trait in the hand with the digivolution cost reduced by 2.`
3. Inherited: `[All Turns] This Digimon gets +1000 DP.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-053` → `(no knowledge-base entries)`. KB-INDEX lists
`none`, so there are no Q&A ids to cover.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[CS] trait: Cost 2` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 3, traits: ["CS"], cost: 2, isAlternate: true }]` | public `digivolve` from BT3-059 (printed Black Lv.3 recipe, 2 memory, stack identity, bonus draw) and from BT22-008 (off-colour Lv.3 CS, alternate recipe, 2 memory); negatives: off-colour Lv.3 without CS (BT1-029) and a Lv.4 CS source, both `invalid-evolution` with memory and hand untouched |
| `When any of your Option cards are placed in the battle area` | `YourTurn` → `SubTrigger whenOptionPlayed`, `sourceFilter: { controller: "mine", kind: ["Option"] }` | public `playCard` of BT23-100 Hudie Net Café, whose printed `[Main]` body places itself in the battle area — the Option really becomes a seat-0 battle-area permanent in every positive case |
| `may … this Digimon digivolve` (optional) | `Digivolve` action, `optional: true`, `target.isSelfRef` | `autoDeclineOptional`: the Option is placed, Strikedramon stays BT23-053 with an empty stack, the hand card stays, and only the Option's own cost 3 is paid |
| `into a Digimon card with [Cyberdramon] in its name or the [CS] trait` | `into.nameOrTrait: [{tokens:["Cyberdramon"], match:"name"}, {tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}]` | name branch: BT23-055 Cyberdramon; trait branch: BT22-037 (Yellow Lv.5 CS) — its only fitting recipe is `Lv.4 w/[CS]: Cost 3`, so the charge pins the branch; a hand with neither raises no decision at all (`s.decisions` empty, no `actionRejected`) |
| `from the hand` | `from: ["hand"]` | the evolved card leaves the hand, the untouched neutral stays |
| `with the digivolution cost reduced by 2` | `reduceCost: 2`, `payCost: true` | exactly 1 memory charged on both branches (3 − 2), against a control test that charges the unreduced 3 for the same evolution without an Option placement |
| still a real digivolution | `Digivolve` action (not a free swap) | hand cards that match the name/trait but have no legal route (BT10-025 Blue Cyberdramon, BT22-064 Lv.6 CS) are refused and stay in hand |
| `[Your Turn]` boundary | `trigger: "YourTurn"` | an own Option placed during the opponent's turn does not fire (turn reached through the real turn loop) |
| `your Option cards` | `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` | an opponent's Option publicly played on the opponent's turn does not fire; an opponent's Option placed while seat 0's Main is open does not fire |
| Inherited `[All Turns] +1000 DP` | `AllTurns`, `isInherited: true`, `ModifyDP +1000`, `isSelfRef`, `duration: "permanent"` | BT23-055 host with BT23-053 underneath reads printed DP + 1000 at setup, on its controller's Main and on the opponent's Main through the real turn loop |

Two cases drive `advance(s.engine).verb.placeOptionAsPermanent` and say so inline: no public
intent places a player's own Option during the opponent's turn, and the opponent cannot
publicly play an Option while seat 0's Main is open. Both are negative boundary cases whose
positive counterparts are proved publicly.

| `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement[1]: { level: 3, traits: ["CS"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }` | public `digivolve` from BT22-043 (Green/Yellow Lv.3 CS, colours miss the printed EvoCost) for exactly 3, with stack identity and the draw; a base whose own effect reduces CS digivolution cost (BT23-037) stacks to 2; a plain Blue Lv.3 (BT2-022) falls back to the printed 4 even with the alternate flag; a Red Lv.3 with neither name nor trait is refused |

| `until their turn ends` | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | real turn loop: still armed across the whole opponent turn, gone on the next own Main |
| `[On Play]` and `[When Digivolving]` carry the same body | two effects with identical `Draw` + `Restrict` actions | the public play case proves `[On Play]`; three public digivolve cases prove `[When Digivolving]` |

#### Contract and rules evidence

play cost 7, DP 7000, EvoCost Black Lv.4 for 3.

Printed clauses:

2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 5 or less.`
3. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon would leave the battle area, by trashing 1 of your Option cards in the battle area, it doesn't leave.`
4. Inherited: `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon with [Cyberdramon] or [Justimon] in its name or the [CS] trait would leave the battle area, by trashing 1 of your Option cards in the battle area, it doesn't leave.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-055` → Q5320 (2025-10-03): the "trash 1 Option card in
the battle area" effect trashes an Option card that was placed there by a "place this card in
the battle area" effect. Covered: the cost filter carries `placedInBattleAreaByEffect: true`,
every fixture places its Option through BT23-100 Hudie Net Café's own public `[Main]` body,
and a dedicated test proves Option cards in hand and in trash are not usable as the cost.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 4, traits: ["CS"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }]` | public `digivolve` from ST5-06 (printed Black Lv.4 route) for 3 with stack identity and the bonus draw; from BT23-041 (off-colour Lv.4 CS) for 3 on both `useAlternateCost` branches; a Lv.4 that is neither Black nor CS (BT1-037) is refused `invalid-evolution` |
| `[On Play] Delete 1 opponent Digimon with play cost ≤5` | `OnPlay` → `Delete`, `controller: "opponent"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, `playCostLte: 5` | public `playCard` for 7: the cost-5 Digimon is deleted and lands in the opponent's trash, the cost-6 one survives, own board and memory endpoint asserted; an opponent whose only Digimon is in breeding loses nothing |
| `[When Digivolving]` same deletion | `WhenDigivolving` → identical `Delete` | public `digivolve` deletes the cost-5 opponent while charging 3 and drawing 1 |
| `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon would leave … it doesn't leave` (printed) | `AllTurns`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`, `Replacement` on `wouldLeavePlay`, `sourceFilter.isSelfRef`, `cost.kind: "trash"` | public attack into a 10000 blocker: Cyberdramon loses the battle, stays in the battle area, exactly one placed Option is trashed and the spare stays on the board |
| `by trashing 1 of your Option cards in the battle area` (Q5320) | cost target `zone: "battleArea"`, `controller: "mine"`, `kind: ["Option"]`, `placedInBattleAreaByEffect: true` | with Option copies only in hand and in trash the same lost battle deletes Cyberdramon and leaves both Option cards where they were |
| `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | on the opponent's turn (reached through the real turn loop) a first attack is prevented at the cost of one Option and a second attack deletes Cyberdramon with the spare Option still on the board — the gate is the frequency, not a missing cost; a separate test spends the use on the controller's own turn and proves the opponent's turn grants a fresh one |
| `[All Turns]` | same effect, no turn gate | proved by the prevention firing on the controller's turn and on the opponent's turn in different tests |
| Inherited clause | second `AllTurns` effect, `isInherited: true`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`, `sourceFilter` with `isSelfRef` + `nameOrTrait` name/trait tokens | BT23-057 Gankoomon carrying BT23-055 as a digivolution card survives a removal at the cost of one placed Option; the host gets exactly one prevention per turn, not one per clause (printed + inherited do not stack); another own CS Digimon and the opponent's CS Digimon are not protected |

The inherited-clause cases drive `advance(s.engine).verb.deletePermanent(..., "byEffect")`
and say so inline: no public intent deletes a player's own Digimon outright. The printed
clause's positive, its Q5320 boundary and its once-per-turn gate are all public-flow.

### BT23-056 — WereGarurumon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` BT23-056: Black, Digimon, Lv.5, play cost 7,
7000 DP, evoCosts Black Lv.4 cost 3, forms Ultimate, attributes Virus, types Beastkin + CS.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3` (alternate route).
2. `＜Blocker＞`.
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] If you have a Tamer with the [CS] trait, give 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon "[Start of Your Main Phase] This Digimon attacks." until their turn ends.`
4. Inherited: `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When attack targets change, ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of
   your opponent's Digimon.`
KB (`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-056`): 1 entry, Q5321 — the grant may be given to a
Digimon that effects don't affect, but the gained effect will not trigger if that Digimon is
still unaffected at the trigger timing. Covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| ＜Blocker＞ | `Static` → `GainKeyword{Blocker, permanent}` | "blocks a real attack aimed at its controller" — public `attack` + `declareBlock`, security untouched, attacker trashed, blocker suspended |
| On Play grant | `OnPlay` → `SubTrigger{startOfYourMainPhase}` on 1 opponent Digimon, `condition youHave Tamer + [CS] trait`, `duration untilOpponentTurnEnd`, body `Attack{self}` | "grants the chosen opponent Digimon a forced attack that fires in their own main phase" — public `playCard` inside `startTurnLoop`, victim suspends and checks security on its own turn |
| "If you have a Tamer with the [CS] trait" | `condition: youHave { kind:["Tamer"], nameOrTrait:[{tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}] }` | "grants nothing without a Tamer that carries the [CS] trait" — no subscription, no forced attack, security intact |
| "until their turn ends" | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` | same test, second half — subscription gone after the victim's own turn, next main phase passes with no attack |
| When Digivolving grant | duplicate `WhenDigivolving` body | "digivolves off-colour from a Lv.4 [CS] source for 3 and grants from that route" — public `digivolve` with `useAlternateCost` |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/[CS]: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:4, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | off-colour Lv.4 CS route (memory 3→0, stack identity, bonus draw) plus printed Black Lv.4 route plus an illegal Blue non-CS negative (`invalid-evolution`, memory unchanged) |
| Inherited De-Digivolve 1 on target switch, Once Per Turn | `AllTurns` `isInherited` `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` → `SubTrigger{whenAttackTargetSwitched}` → `DeDigivolve 1` | "de-digivolves once per turn when a real block switches an attack target, and resets next turn" — real attacks and real `declareBlock`s drive the target switch; same-turn refusal and next-own-turn reset asserted with shed card ids in the trash |
| Q5321 | same grant path plus the engine's `beAffected` restriction | "grants onto an unaffected Digimon, which then never triggers the gained attack (Q5321)" — grant installed, immunity live at the trigger timing, no attack, security intact |
All proofs are public-flow. No injected timing, no verbs.

### BT23-057 — Gankoomon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog BT23-057: Black, Digimon, Lv.6, play cost 11, 11000 DP, evoCosts Black Lv.5 cost 3 and
Red Lv.5 cost 3, Mega, Data, types Holy Warrior + Royal Knight + CS. No inherited text.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`.
2. `When this card would be played, by returning 3 cards with [Huckmon], [Sistermon] or [Jesmon]
   in their names from your trash to the top or bottom of the deck, reduce the play cost by 5.`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] You may play 1 [Hinukamuy] Token. (Digimon/White/6000 DP/
   ＜Alliance＞ ＜Reboot＞ ＜Blocker＞) Then, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost
   of 6 or less. For each of your other Digimon, add 3 to this effect's play cost maximum.`
KB: 1 entry, Q5322 — returning only 2 of the 3 cards cannot satisfy the "by" condition. Covered.
The token definition is shared data (`packages/shared/src/cards/tokens.ts`, `TOKEN-Hinukamuy-Token`,
White / 6000 DP / Alliance / Reboot / Blocker) and matches the printed parenthetical.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| "would be played … by returning 3 named cards … to the top or bottom of the deck, reduce by 5" | `Static` → `Replacement{wouldBePlayed, mode:"reduceCost", amount:5}` with `cost{kind:"return", to:"deckTopOrBottom", target zone:"trash", nameOrTrait match:"name", count:3}` | parameterised "reduces the play cost by 5 by returning three named cards from the trash to the %s of the deck" (top and bottom) — public `playCard`, memory 11→5, the three named cards land at the chosen end, the unrelated trash card stays |
| Q5322 (partial return is not a payment) | `count: 3` on the return cost | "refuses the reduction when only two matching cards sit in the trash (Q5322)" — full 11 paid, trash unchanged, deck unchanged |
| the reduction is a "may" | `Replacement` cost declined | "pays the full cost when the return cost is declined" — memory 11→0, trash intact |
| "You may play 1 [Hinukamuy] Token" | `PlayToken{Hinukamuy Token, count 1, payCost false, optional true}` | accept path asserts the token exists with 6000 DP and live Alliance / Reboot / Blocker through `observe().hasKeyword`; decline path asserts no token |
| "Then, delete 1 … with a play cost of 6 or less" (mandatory) | `Delete{optional:false, filter playCostLte:6}` | "deletes only up to the base play-cost ceiling of 6 when the token is declined" — cost-6 body deleted, cost-7 body survives |
| "For each of your other Digimon, add 3 to this effect's play cost maximum" | `playCostCeiling{base:6, raise:3, per:1, filter{controller:"mine", zone:"battleArea", excludeSelf:true, kind:["Digimon"]}}` placed before `Delete` | "raises the deletion ceiling by 3 for the token and by 3 for each other own Digimon" — token + ally lift the ceiling to 12, a cost-11 body dies, a cost-15 body survives |
| zone scoping of "your other Digimon" | `zone: "battleArea"` on the ceiling filter | "does not count a breeding-area Digimon toward the deletion ceiling" — ceiling stays 6, a cost-7 body survives |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS]: Cost 3` and the When Digivolving body | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]`, duplicated `WhenDigivolving` actions | "digivolves from a Lv.5 [CS] source for the alternate cost of 3 and draws 1" — Blue AeroVeedramon source is off both printed colour rows, memory 4→1, source kept in `stack`, bonus draw, token + deletion resolve |
| illegal source | same requirement | "rejects a Lv.5 source without the [CS] trait on both routes" — `invalid-evolution` on the alternate and printed routes, memory unchanged |
| IR shape | — | "keeps the printed IR shape for the reduction and the token-then-delete body" (structural, supplementary only) |
All behavioural proofs are public-flow (`playCard` / `digivolve`). No injected timing, no verbs,
no direct state mutation beyond `s.state.memory`.

### BT23-058 — Craniamon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog BT23-058: Black, Digimon, Lv.6, play cost 11, 11000 DP, evoCosts Black Lv.5 cost 3, Mega,
Vaccine, types Holy Warrior + Royal Knight + CS, rarity R, max 4. No inherited text.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`.
2. `＜Reboot＞`, 3. `＜Blocker＞`.
4. `[All Turns] When any of your Digimon or Tamers would leave the battle area by your opponent's
   effects, by suspending this Digimon, 1 of those Digimon or Tamers doesn't leave.`
5. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon suspends, delete all of your opponent's Digimon
   with the lowest play cost.`
KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-058` → no entries. Every assertion is anchored on the
printed text and the comprehensive rules.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| ＜Reboot＞ | `Static` keyword `Reboot` | "Reboot unsuspends this Digimon during the opponent's Active phase" — Craniamon suspends by a real attack on its own turn (after its own Active phase), then the opponent's Active phase unsuspends it; the turn handover runs through `startTurnLoop` |
| ＜Blocker＞ | `Static` keyword `Blocker` | "Blocker redirects an opposing attack and the block suspension deletes the lowest-cost opponents" — real seat-1 turn, real attack, `blockWindowOpened` lists only Craniamon, real `declareBlock`, security intact, attacker trashed |
| "When any of your Digimon or Tamers would leave … by your opponent's effects, by suspending this Digimon, 1 doesn't leave" | `AllTurns` → `Replacement{wouldLeavePlay, mode:"prevent", leaveCause:"byOpponentEffect", sourceFilter/target controller:"mine", kind:["Digimon","Tamer"], count 1}` with `cost{kind:"suspend", isSelfRef}` | "suspends to save an ally from an opposing Option and chains into the lowest-cost deletion" — the opponent plays BT2-091 Volcanic Flare on its own turn; the ally survives, seat 0's trash stays empty, Craniamon suspends |
| "1 of those" (exactly one) | `count: 1` | "saves only one of two allies threatened by one opposing Option" — the opponent plays BT6-095 (delete all with the lowest DP) against two tied 4000 DP allies; exactly one survives, one reaches the trash |
| "by your opponent's effects" | `leaveCause: "byOpponentEffect"` | "does not protect against your own effects" — seat 0's own BT7-107 deletes its own Digimon; the ally leaves and Craniamon stays unsuspended |
| the protection is optional | replacement cost declined | "may decline the protection and lets the ally leave with this Digimon unsuspended" — ally trashed, Craniamon unsuspended, no sweep |
| the cost must be payable | `cost{kind:"suspend"}` | "cannot pay the protection cost once a block has already suspended it" — a real block suspends Craniamon first, then the opposing Option resolves unopposed |
| "When this Digimon suspends, delete all of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest play cost" | `AllTurns` `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` → `SubTrigger{whenSuspended, sourceFilter isSelfRef}` → `Delete{superlative:"lowestPlayCost", count:"all"}` | "deletes every tied lowest-play-cost opponent in the battle area and spares breeding" — real attack suspension, both tied cost-3 bodies deleted, the cheaper breeding card untouched; also chained from every protection and block proof above |
| `[Once Per Turn]` | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` | "fires the suspend deletion once per turn and re-arms it on the next own turn" — first attack deletes, a second suspend in the same turn does not, the counter resets through the real turn loop |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS]: Cost 3` | `digivolutionRequirement [{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}]` | "takes the alternate Lv.5 [CS] route for 3 and draws, and refuses non-CS or wrong-level sources" — off-colour Lv.5 CS route (memory 0→-3, source kept in `stack`, bonus draw), printed Black Lv.5 route, and two illegal-source negatives |
| IR shape | — | "compiles the leave-play replacement and the once-per-turn suspend deletion" (structural, supplementary only) |

  → `waitForMainPhase(1)`, then the opponent plays a real Option (`BT2-091` Volcanic Flare,

### BT23-059 — Justimon: Blitz Arm

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog BT23-059: Black, Digimon, Lv.6, play cost 11, 11000 DP, evoCosts Black Lv.5 cost 3, Mega,
Vaccine, types Cyborg + Hudie + CS. No inherited text.
Printed clauses:
1. `[Digivolve] [Justimon: Accel Arm]/[Justimon: Critical Arm]: Cost 1`.
2. `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS] trait: Cost 3`.
3. `＜Blocker＞`.
4. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] [When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] By trashing 1 Option card in the
   battle area, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with the lowest play cost.`
5. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When Option cards in the battle area are trashed, this Digimon
   unsuspends. Then, your opponent's Digimon's effects don't affect this Digimon for the turn.`
KB: 7 entries, Q5323–Q5329.
- Q5323 (the cost trashes an Option placed by a "place this card in the battle area" effect):
  tested — `placedInBattleAreaByEffect: true`, and the fixture is a real BT23-100 Hudie Net Cafe
  played publicly. Also proved against an Option in the OPPONENT's battle area.
- Q5324 ("effects don't affect" means the printed consequence never lands): tested — a chosen
  -3000 DP leaves the DP at 11000.
- Q5325 (an unaffected card can still be chosen): tested.
- Q5327 (gaining the immunity ends an effect already affecting the card): tested.
- Q5326, Q5328, Q5329: documented, not tested. They are the same ruling read from three other
  angles (a granted keyword is not counted; the effect applies again once the immunity drops; a
  granted trigger does not fire while the immunity is up). None is reachable from this card by a
  public flow: the immunity lasts only "for the turn" and covers only the opponent's DIGIMON
  effects, and no opponent Digimon effect in the corpus grants this card a keyword or a trigger
  inside that same turn. The shared seam all three run through
  (`isRestrictedByEffect(..., "beAffected", "Digimon")`) is asserted in the Q5324, Q5325 and Q5327
  cases. The reason is recorded as a comment above the Q5324 test.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| ＜Blocker＞ | `Static` keyword `Blocker` | "exposes Blocker through the live keyword seam and blocks a real attack" |
| "By trashing 1 Option card in the battle area, delete 1 … with the lowest play cost" | `OnPlay` / `WhenDigivolving` / `WhenAttacking` → `Delete{superlative:"lowestPlayCost"}` with `cost{kind:"trash", filter zone:"battleArea", kind:["Option"], placedInBattleAreaByEffect:true}` | "trashes the publicly placed Option when digivolving and deletes the lowest-play-cost opponent"; "unsuspends itself mid-attack when its When Attacking effect trashes the Option" |
| the cost is a genuine "may" on a triggered effect | `optional:true, abortOnDecline:true` | "declining the optional cost leaves the Option, the opponent and the immunity untouched" |
| no payable cost | same | "does nothing when no Option is in the battle area to pay the cost" |
| Q5323 scope | `placedInBattleAreaByEffect: true`, no controller filter | "accepts an Option in the OPPONENT's battle area as the trash cost" |
| `[Once Per Turn]` shared across the three timings | `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`, `sharedUseKey:"ir-shared-0"` | "uses the shared Once Per Turn only once a turn and resets it on the next own turn" (real turn loop) |
| "When Option cards … are trashed, this Digimon unsuspends" | `AllTurns` `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` → `SubTrigger{whenOptionInBattleAreaTrashed}` → `Unsuspend{self}` | "once per turn unsuspends and protects itself…" plus the mid-attack unsuspend proof |
| "Then, your opponent's Digimon's effects don't affect this Digimon for the turn" | `GrantStatic{immuneToOpponentDigimonEffects, duration:"forTheTurn"}` | "ignores an opponent Digimon's DP reduction once the Option trash grants immunity (Q5324)" — paired runs with and without the Option: 8000 DP versus 11000 DP; "stays a choosable but unaffected target while the immunity is up (Q5325)"; "ends an opponent Digimon's DP reduction the moment it gains the immunity (Q5327)" |
| `[Digivolve] [Justimon: Accel Arm]/[Justimon: Critical Arm]: Cost 1` | `digivolutionRequirement [{namesExact:[…], cost:1, isAlternate:true}]` | "digivolves for 1 from either named Justimon, for 3 from an off-colour Lv.5 CS and from a Black Lv.5" |
| `[Digivolve] Lv.5 w/[CS]: Cost 3` | `{level:5, traits:["CS"], cost:3, isAlternate:true}` | same test, plus "refuses an illegal source" |
Every behavioural proof is public-flow (`playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack`, `declareBlock`,
security checks). No injected timing, no verbs, no `turnSeat` writes.

- `BT23-059.ts`: the named-source evolution row used `names:` (substring). `[Name]` is exact, so it

  the change removes the silent substring widening.
- `BT23-059.test.ts`: matching expectation updated, plus Q&A labelling — the Q5323 case and the

Then, delete 1 of their Digimon with 8000 DP or less" | `OnPlay` / `WhenDigivolving` → `DeDigivolve{opponent Digimon, amount 1}` then `Delete{opponent Digimon, dp lte 8000}` | "de-digivolves first, then deletes the resulting 8000-DP-or-lower Digimon" — public `playCard`, 13000 DP GranDracmon sheds down to 4000 DP Sangloupmon and is then deleted while a 14000 DP body survives; "plays for 12, de-digivolves first, then deletes only the opponent's resulting 4000-DP Digimon" (own small Digimon untouched) |

| "Activate 1 [On Play] effect on a face-up [Zaxon] Digimon card in your security stack as an effect of this Digimon" | `WhenAttacking` → `ActivateForeignEffect{zone:"security", fromTriggers:["OnPlay"], filter controller:"mine", faceUp:true, trait "Zaxon", count 1}` | "activates a face-up Zaxon security card's On Play effect when attacking" (real attack); negatives for a face-down Zaxon card, a face-up non-Zaxon card and the opponent's face-up Zaxon card |

### BT23-062 — Dracmon

#### Contract and rules evidence

Catalog: Purple Lv.3 Rookie/Virus, traits Undead / CS, play cost 3, DP 1000, evoCost Purple Lv.2 cost 0.

Printed clauses:
- C1 `[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[CS] trait: Cost 0`
- C2 `[Start of Your Main Phase] By trash 1 card with the [Undead], [Dark Animal] or [CS] trait from your hand, gain 1 memory.`
- C3 inherited `[When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] 1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a Digimon card with the [Undead] or [Dark Animal] trait in the trash.`

KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-062` → Q5333 only. Covered.
Rules used: CR 15-7-1 / 15-7-2 / 15-7-4 (optional processing conditions — "By X, Y" is the player's choice, and declining blocks the payload); CR 15-8-4-4-1 is the *activation-type* rule and does not apply to a `[Start of Your Main Phase]` trigger; CR 15-16-5-1 for Q5333.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| identity | catalog record | `matches every catalog field and printed text` (structural) |
| C1 | `digivolutionRequirement:[{level:2,traits:["CS"],cost:0,isAlternate:true}]` | `digivolves from a level 2 CS source of another colour for 0` (both `useAlternateCost` branches; BT23-003 Motimon is Black, so only the [CS] clause makes it legal) and `refuses a level 2 source that is neither purple nor CS` (both branches) — public `digivolve` |
| C2 cost + payload | `StartOfYourMainPhase` → `GainMemory 1` with `cost.kind:"trash"` over hand cards matching `["Undead","Dark Animal","CS"]` (`match:"trait"`), `optional:true`, `abortOnDecline:true` | `gains 1 memory at the start of your own main phase by trashing a matching hand card`; `accepts %s as the %s trait cost` (one case per printed trait: BT23-066 Undead, BT23-063 Dark Animal, BT22-008 CS); **new** `keeps the hand card and gains nothing when the controller declines the trash cost`; `does not gain memory when no hand card carries a matching trait` — all through the real turn loop |
| C2 controller/turn boundary | `StartOfYourMainPhase` | `stays silent on the opponent's main phase and fires again on your next turn` — three real main phases, trash contents asserted at each |
| C3 | inherited `WhenAttacking`, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"`, `Digivolve from:["trash"]`, `payCost:true`, `optional:true` | `digivolves a carrier into an Undead card from the trash when attacking`; `leaves the board untouched when the optional inherited digivolution is declined`; `is once per turn and resets on your next turn` (real turn loop; the same-turn second attack is arranged with `advance().verb.unsuspend`, which is arrangement only — the refusal itself is a real `attack` intent) |
| Q5333 | engine timing | `does not activate the newly inherited When Attacking effect after digivolving mid-window (Q5333)` — BT24-069 Vilemon has an inherited-only `[When Attacking]` ("trash the top card of both decks"); neither deck loses a card. Companion: `still resolves its own inherited effect after the top card's When Attacking digivolution` pins the legal neighbouring case |

#### Corrections

**Module `BT23-062.ts`** — added `optional: true` to the `StartOfYourMainPhase` action.
The clause is a trigger-type effect with an optional processing condition ("By trashing …, gain 1 memory"). CR 15-7-4 gives the player the choice whether to execute it; CR 15-8-4-4-1 (cost mandatory once declared) applies only to activation-type ([Main]) effects, so it does not make this cost mandatory. `abortOnDecline` is documented in `packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/base.ts:38-42` as applying to **an optional action**, so without `optional: true` it was inert and the trash was forced. Peer `apps/api/src/cards/BT11/BT11-092.ts:25-26` has the identical printed shape and carries `optional: true` + `abortOnDecline: true`.

**Test `BT23-062.test.ts`**
- Renamed and retargeted the IR-shape test to assert `optional: true` (was asserting `optional` undefined, i.e. pinning the defect).
- Added the behavioral decline test named above.
- Added `autoAcceptOptional: true` to the four accept-path main-phase fixtures, which now must answer the new prompt. Without the module change, the new decline test fails (the cost is paid and memory becomes 1).

#### Commands and results

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/BT23/BT23-062.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` → `Test Files 1 passed (1)` / `Tests 19 passed (19)`.
- oxlint clean; oxfmt `--check` clean; `git diff --check` clean.
- Rejection grep returns nothing.
- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` → no diagnostic mentions `BT23-062`.

#### Retained reds and gaps

None (no `it.fails`, no skips). Gaps:
- The alternate route's `cost: 0` equals the printed route's `cost: 0`, so the two are separated only by colour legality, never by memory.
- `[Once Per Turn]` is proved per source card on one carrier; two separate Dracmon copies each holding their own use is not tested (no ruling asks for it).
- The same-turn second attack needs `advance().verb.unsuspend` because no public route unsuspends an own Digimon mid-turn without importing another card's effect.

#### Catalog discrepancies

The catalog prints "By trash 1 card" where the official card list reads "By trashing 1 card" (field `effectText`, BT23-062). The test asserts the committed catalog string verbatim; the compiled `raw` matches it. Cosmetic; no behavioural consequence. Catalog is not editable in this lane.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| C2 both trait branches + cost | `into.nameOrTrait [Undead, CS] match:"trait"`, `from:["trash"]`, `payCost:true` | `digivolves itself out of the trash on %s while attacking, paying its cost` (Undead for 2, CS for 3), exact stack, memory and remaining security asserted |

| Q5332 | engine timing | `Q5332 does not activate its own inherited effect after the printed one digivolves it` — the Lv.6 [Dark Animal] reachable only by the inherited clause stays in the trash |
| Q5333 | engine timing | `Q5333 does not activate its inherited effect after BT23-062 digivolves it` — same proof on the ruling's literal board (BT23-063 over BT23-062) |
| C3 both trait branches | inherited `into.nameOrTrait [Undead, Dark Animal]` | `inherited: digivolves another of your Digimon into %s from the trash` (Undead for 3, Dark Animal for 2) |

#### Corrections

Test file only; the module IR was already correct.

1. `inherited: refuses a second use in the same turn and works again on the next own turn` — replaced the hand-rolled sequence (`s.state.turnSeat = 1` → `runTurn(1)` → `s.state.turnSeat = 0` → `runOneTurn()` → `s.perm("attacker").isSuspended = false`) with `startTurnLoop()` plus `waitForMainPhase`/`endMainPhaseIfOpen` across seat 0 → seat 1 → seat 0, ending on `surrender`. The attacker is now unsuspended by the real Unsuspend Phase, and that is asserted (`isSuspended === false`). Memory is arranged only after the phase opens. Both direct-mutation writes are gone.
2. `does not fire on the opponent's attack` — removed `s.state.turnSeat = 1` and the `suspended: true` seat-0 fixture (undone by `startTurnLoop`'s Unsuspend Phase anyway). The test now reaches seat 1's real main phase through the loop without Sangloupmon ever attacking, and seat 1 attacks the player. Endpoints asserted: Sangloupmon still the top card with an empty stack, seat 0's security down to 1, the trash card untouched, no pending decision.

Net: the three `turnSeat` writes flagged by the coordinator and one manual `isSuspended` write are removed; the same clauses are now proved through public flow.

### BT23-068 — GranDracmon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog entry BT23-068: Purple Lv.6 Mega, Virus, `[Dark Animal]`/`[CS]`, play cost 13, DP 13000,
printed evo Purple Lv.5 for 5.
Printed clauses:

2. `[Start of Your Main Phase] [On Deletion] 1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a level 6 or
   lower [Undead] or [Dark Animal] trait Digimon card in the trash without paying the cost.`
3. `[When Digivolving] You may play 1 level 4 or lower purple Digimon card from your trash without
   paying the cost.`
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When any of your Digimon digivolve from the trash, delete all of
   your opponent's lowest level Digimon.`
KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-068` → Q5336 ("Does this card's [All Turns] effect also
trigger when any of my Digimon digivolves into this card from the trash?" → "Yes, it triggers.").

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Alternate `Lv.5 w/[Undead]/[CS]: Cost 4` | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` `{level:5, traits:["Undead","CS"], cost:4, isAlternate:true}` | "charges the printed Purple Lv.5 route 5 memory"; "charges 4 on the alternate [Undead]/[CS] route and keeps the base under the stack" (stack identity + digivolution draw); "takes the alternate route from a Lv.5 with only the [CS] trait"; "denies the cost-4 route to a Lv.5 purple base with neither [Undead] nor [CS]"; "rejects an off-colour Lv.5 [Dark Animal] source outright" (`invalid-evolution`). Public flow |
| `[Start of Your Main Phase]` free trash digivolve | `StartOfYourMainPhase` → `Digivolve`, `into` `lte 6` + `match:"trait"` `["Undead","Dark Animal"]`, `from:["trash"]`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true` | "digivolves a Digimon into the trash [Dark Animal] card for free and skips the off-trait card" (mixed trash pool: qualifying, off-trait, trait-matching-but-unreachable); "leaves the board untouched when the controller declines"; "does not fire on the opponent's main phase". Public flow (real turn loop) |
| `[On Deletion]` same free trash digivolve | `OnDeletion` → identical `Digivolve` | **New this follow-up:** "fires when the opponent deletes it with an Option on their own turn". GranDracmon is digivolved in from hand during seat 0's main (so its own `[Start of Your Main Phase]` has already passed and cannot consume the trash card), the turn passes through the real turn loop, and seat 1 plays ST1-16 Gaia Force ("[Main] Delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon"). Endpoints asserted: GranDracmon in seat 0's trash; the Lv.4 base now topped by the trash `[Undead]` card with the base preserved in `stack`; the qualifying card gone from the trash; the off-trait `[Ghost]` card still in it; memory 2 (only Gaia Force's cost of 8 was paid, so the Lv.4→Lv.5 route of 2 was free); no pending decision. Public flow |
| `[When Digivolving]` free Lv.<=4 purple play from trash | `WhenDigivolving` → `PlayWithoutCost`, `colors:["Purple"]`, `lte 4`, `from:["trash"]`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true` | "plays a level 4 or lower purple Digimon from the trash and leaves the Lv.5 behind" (memory endpoint proves the play was free); "leaves the trash alone when the controller declines"; the On Deletion test adds a negative — a trash holding only Lv.5 cards leaves the board at two permanents. Public flow |
| `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn]` delete all lowest-level on a trash digivolve | `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger whenOneOfYoursDigivolves`, `fireCondition digivolvedFromZone trash`, `Delete` `superlative:"lowestLevel"`, `count:"all"`, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` | "does not delete when the watched evolution comes from hand"; "triggers its own effect when a Digimon digivolves into it from the trash (Q5336)" — the card's own `[Start of Your Main Phase]` digivolves a Lv.5 into a second BT23-068 out of the trash, with a Lv.3/Lv.3/Lv.4/Lv.5 opposing board so the level-4 deletion is attributable only to the newly arrived copy; **rewritten this follow-up:** "fires on the opponent's turn" and "fires once per turn and resets on the next own turn" (details below). All public flow |

#### Corrections
Test only; the module is byte-identical to `HEAD`.
1. Q5336 test: replaced the injected `advance(...).verb.deletePermanent` arrangement with the
   card's own `[Start of Your Main Phase]` digivolution out of the trash into a second BT23-068,
   and added a level-4 opponent so the newcomer's firing is separable from the existing watcher's.
2. New `[On Deletion]` test (see the clause table). The clause previously had **no** test at all —
   my first report wrongly described the Q5336 `deletePermanent` call as the On Deletion proof.
   Mutation check: flipping the module's `trigger: "OnDeletion"` to `"OnPlay"` fails this test.
3. "fires on the opponent's turn": now fully public. Two GranDracmon are digivolved in from hand
   during seat 0's main phase (neither `[Start of Your Main Phase]` clause ever runs, so the trash
   card survives); on seat 1's turn Gaia Force deletes one of them, its `[On Deletion]` digivolves
   the Lv.4 base out of the trash, and the **second** GranDracmon — still on the board — is the
   watcher that fires. The opponent's level 3 is deleted and the level 4 red anchor survives, so
   the "lowest level only" boundary is asserted on the opponent's turn.
4. "fires once per turn and resets on the next own turn": the second same-turn trash digivolution
   no longer uses `advance(...).verb.digivolveFromInstance`. The BT23-063 Sangloupmon that arrives
   from the first digivolution attacks, and its printed `[When Attacking]` ("This Digimon may
   digivolve into a Digimon card with the [Undead] or [CS] trait in the trash") digivolves it into
   an inert BT2-075 Myotismon — a second trash digivolution in the same turn, through a public
   `attack` intent. The delete is held, the opponent's board is re-asserted after the opponent's
   whole turn, and the third digivolution on the next own turn deletes again. Mutation check:
   removing `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` from the module fails this test.

The file now contains **no** `advance(...).verb`, `advance(...).fire`, `fireTiming`,
`fireSubTrigger`, `turnSeat =` or numeric `security: <n>` usage. Every printed clause has a
public-flow proof.

→ `Test Files  1 passed (1)` / `Tests  16 passed (16)`.

None. No `it.fails`, no injected timing, no structural-only clause. Q5336 has a behavioural test.

Two rules notes worth carrying into the ledger:
- When a Digimon digivolves out of the trash into a BT23-068, **both** the copy already on the
  board and the newly arrived copy fire their own `[All Turns]` watcher on that one event (each has
  its own once-per-turn counter). Q5336 establishes the newcomer's firing; the Q5336 test separates
  the two by level.
- A GranDracmon deleted by an opponent's effect fires its own `[On Deletion]` digivolution, but it
  is no longer in the battle area when that digivolution resolves, so its own `[All Turns]` watcher
  does not see it. The opponent's-turn test therefore needs a second GranDracmon as the watcher.

### BT23-069 — Necromon

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog entry BT23-069: Purple Lv.6 Mega, Virus, `[Ghost]`/`[LIBERATOR]`, play cost 11, DP 11000,
printed evo Purple Lv.5 for 3. No alternate digivolve line, and the module correctly carries no
`digivolutionRequirement`.
Printed clauses:
1. `＜Execute＞`.
2. `[On Play] [On Deletion] You may play 1 level 5 or lower Digimon card with the [Ghost] trait
   from your trash without paying the cost.`
3. `[All Turns] When another Digimon attacks, by deleting this Digimon, delete 1 of your opponent's
   level 6 or lower Digimon. If this effect didn't delete your opponent's Digimon, you may end that
   attack.`
KB: `node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-069` → Q5337, Q5338, Q5339, Q5340. All four are covered:
- Q5337 (cannot decline into "didn't delete" when an eligible target exists) — "never offers to skip
  the opponent deletion when an eligible target exists (Q5337)", which asserts that the only optional
  prompt this card raises is the `by deleting this Digimon` cost.
- Q5338 (a deletion-immune chosen target still satisfies "didn't delete") — "ends the attack when
  the only eligible target is immune to the deletion", using BT14-062.
- Q5339 / Q5340 ("end the attack" changes the timing, not the Digimon) — the same test asserts the
  jump straight to end-of-attack: no security check, no block window, attacker still in play.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `＜Execute＞` | `Static` → `GainKeyword Execute` on self, `duration:"permanent"` | "exposes Execute through the live keyword seam". Also exercised for real: during the turn hand-over the engine raises Execute's `[End of Your Turn]` attack, which the new turn-loop helper declines by name |
| `[On Play]` free Lv.<=5 `[Ghost]` play from trash | `OnPlay` → `PlayWithoutCost`, `lte 5`, `match:"trait"` `["Ghost"]`, `from:["trash"]`, `payCost:false`, `optional:true` | "plays a level 5 or lower [Ghost] Digimon from the trash on play without paying its cost"; "leaves the Ghost in the trash when the On Play optional is declined"; "plays nothing when the trash holds no level 5 or lower [Ghost] Digimon" (mixed trash pool: too-high level and off-trait). Public flow |
| `[On Deletion]` same free play | `OnDeletion` → identical `PlayWithoutCost` | Proved as a side effect of the `[All Turns]` self-deletion in "deletes itself and an eligible opponent, plays a Ghost on deletion, and lets the attack continue". Public flow |
| `When another Digimon attacks` | `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger whenAttacking`, `sourceFilter {excludeSelf:true, kind:["Digimon"]}` | "does not fire on its own attack — the clause reads 'another Digimon'"; "fires on the opponent's attack too and deletes the attacking Digimon" (rewritten this audit onto the real turn loop). Public flow |
| `by deleting this Digimon` (cost) | first `Delete` on self, `optional:true`, `abortOnDecline:true` | "may decline self-deletion, leaving both Digimon in play while the attack proceeds"; "models self-deletion as an optional By processing condition before the opponent deletion". Public flow |
| `delete 1 of your opponent's level 6 or lower Digimon` | second `Delete`, `controller:"opponent"`, `lte 6`, `count:1`, mandatory | "the effect deletes the opponent's Lv.<=6 Digimon (count 1) => the attack CONTINUES"; Q5337 test proves it is never offered as optional. Public flow |
| `If this effect didn't delete …, you may end that attack` | `EndAttack`, `optional:true`, `condition {kind:"ifThisEffectDidNotDelete"}` | "deletes itself and ends the attack when no eligible opponent can be deleted"; "the chosen Lv.<=6 target is deletion-IMMUNE (count 0, Q5338) => the attack ENDS"; "ends the attack when the only eligible target is immune to the deletion (Q5338, Q5339, Q5340)"; "may decline to end the attack, letting it check security instead" (proves the choice was raised and refused, not skipped). Public flow |

#### Corrections
Test only. Replaced the two `s.state.turnSeat = 1` writes with a `passTurnToOpponent` helper that
runs the real turn loop. The helper must decline optionals while the turn changes hands: ＜Execute＞
offers an `[End of Your Turn]` attack whose end-of-attack step deletes Necromon, which under

therefore drop `autoAcceptOptional` and answer their own prompts through the file's existing

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog: White Digimon, no level, play cost 3, DP 1000, no evoCosts, forms Eater, attribute "-",
types [Hudie, CS].

`[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When any of your other Digimon with the [Eater] or [Hudie] trait would
leave the battle area, by deleting this Digimon or placing it as the bottom digivolution card of
your [Mother Eater] in the breeding area, 1 of those Digimon doesn't leave.`;
inherited `[Breeding] [Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When any of your Digimon cards with the [Eater]
trait would be played, you may reduce the play costs by 1.`
KB: Q5349 (this prevention and BT22-007's inherited effect may both be used in sequence — the leave
event has no cause qualifier), Q5350 ({Breeding} effects work only in the breeding area). Both covered.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| [On Play] delete an opponent Lv3 Digimon | `OnPlay Delete`, `filter {controller:"opponent", levels:[3]}` | public `playCard`: memory 3→0, the Lv3 goes to trash, the Lv4 stays, no rejection |
| [All Turns][OPT] another own [Eater]/[Hudie] Digimon would leave → pay a cost, it doesn't leave | `Replacement wouldLeavePlay`, `sourceFilter {controller:"mine", excludeSelf:true, trait Eater/Hudie}`, no `leaveCause` (printed text has no cause qualifier), `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` | opponent-effect cause (their Eater Bit's [On Play] deletion) via the real turn loop; battle-deletion cause; controller's own delayed deletion (BT23-037/BT23-048 Q5565/Q5567), with the "can't digivolve" restriction still on the survivor |
| cost option 1 — delete this Digimon | `costOptions[0] deleteOwn` self | the saved ally stays, Eater Bit's instance is in trash |
| cost option 2 — place it as the bottom digivolution card of your [Mother Eater] in breeding | `costOptions[1] place`, `destination:"digivolutionStack"`, `position:"bottom"`, host breeding + **`match:"nameExact"`** | `preferOptionIndex: 1`: Eater Bit ends at stack index 0 under the breeding Mother Eater, nothing in trash |
| "your OTHER Digimon" | `excludeSelf: true` | Eater Bit loses its own battle and is not saved |
| trait gate | trait tokens Eater/Hudie | a plain level 3 ally is not protected |
| optional | `optional` + `abortOnDecline` | decline → the ally leaves, Eater Bit survives |
| Inherited [Breeding][Your Turn][OPT] reduce an [Eater] play cost by 1 | `YourTurn Replacement wouldBePlayed`, `isInherited`, `isBreeding`, `frequency:"OncePerTurn"` | first Eater costs 2, second costs the full 3 (Q5350 once per turn); Q5350 zone gate — the same stack in the battle area gives no reduction |

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-076` returns no rulings and no errata, matching KB-INDEX.

(`match: "name"`), "the [Royal Knight] or [CS] trait" is exact (`match: "trait"`).
"1 of your other Digimon" excludes Blanc and the breeding area (CR 3-4-5-8).

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Catalog identity and full coverage | `compiled.coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` | "matches every catalog field and declares complete coverage" (structural pin) |
| `[On Play]` add top security to hand | `OnPlay` → `SecurityManipulation op:"toHand" controller:"mine" amount:1 toTop:true` | "on play, adds the top security card to hand and then recovers 1 from the deck on top" — public `playCard`; exact instance ids in hand/security/deck, face-down top, memory 0 |
| Then `＜Recovery +1 (Deck)＞` | `SecurityManipulation op:"addTop" source:"deck" amount:1` | same test: `deckTop` is the new security top and left the deck; order asserted by id |
| `[Your Turn] When this Digimon suspends` | `YourTurn` → `SubTrigger event:"whenSuspended" sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}` | "when Blanc suspends by attacking…" — public `attack` intent is the suspension; "does not react when a different Digimon suspends"; "stays silent on the opponent's turn when an opponent effect suspends Blanc" (P-131 On Play, real turn loop) |
| `1 of your other Digimon may digivolve` | `Digivolve target:{controller:"mine", excludeSelf:true, kind:["Digimon"]}` count 1, `optional: true` | "offers only other battle-area Digimon, never Blanc itself or the breeding Digimon" (candidate list asserted); "declining the optional digivolution leaves the board and memory untouched" |

| `or the [Royal Knight] or [CS] trait` | `{tokens:["Royal Knight","CS"], match:"trait"}` (exact) | "takes a [CS]-trait card from the trash on its printed route for 1 less" (BT22-010); "takes a [Royal Knight]-trait card from hand for 1 less" (BT23-054 Magnamon) |

| `in the hand or trash` | `from: ["hand","trash"]` | hand route (BaoHuckmon, Magnamon) and trash route (BT22-010) both proved |
| `with the digivolution cost reduced by 1` | `payCost: true, reduceCost: 1` | printed route 2→1 (memory 3→2), alternate [CS] route 2→1 (`preferOptionIndex: 1`, memory 5→4), Royal Knight route 4→3; control test "charges the full digivolution cost without Blanc's effect" pins the unreduced 2 |
| No once-per-turn cap | no `frequency` on the `YourTurn` effect | "triggers again on a second suspension in the same turn" — two real attacks with a public unsuspension in between (BT10-110 Seiken Meppa `[Main] Unsuspend 1 of your Digimon`) |

4. Official "(Rule)" line, absent from the catalog `effectText`: this card is also treated as
   `[Sistermon Noir]` and has the Virus attribute.

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-077` returns no rulings and no errata. Q&A ids to cover: none.

Rule-line evidence: `docs/audits/BT23.md` records the session-2 check against
`logs/official-bt23.html` — BT23-077 carries the `[Sistermon Noir]` name alias plus the Virus
attribute. ST12-13 Sistermon Ciel prints the same sentence inside its catalog text
("*Also treat as if name is [Sistermon Noir] and traits include [Virus]"), which corroborates it
independently of the audit log. BT6-084 Sistermon Ciel carries the identical `Rule` IR shape.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof

| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
|---|---|---|
| Catalog identity, exact effect text, full coverage | `coverage: "full"`, `residual: []` | "matches every catalog field and complete compiled clause" (structural pin of all four IR effects) |
| `＜Blocker＞` | `Static` effect, `keywords: [{keyword:"Blocker", raw:"＜Blocker＞"}]` | "projects Blocker through the live continuous ledger" (`observe().hasKeyword`) and, publicly, "blocks a declared attack and de-digivolves during the opponent's turn" — real `declareBlock`, no `securityChecked`, security still 2 |
| `[On Play] Delete 1 … play cost 4 or less` | `OnPlay` → `Delete target:{controller:"opponent", kind:["Digimon"], playCostLte: 4}` count 1 | "deletes exactly the play-cost-4 opposing Digimon when played publicly" — public `playCard`; victim instance id in the opponent trash, the cost-5 peer untouched, memory 6→2, no pending decision |
| Boundary: play cost 5 | same `playCostLte: 4` | "leaves an opposing play-cost-5 Digimon alone and asks nothing" — trash empty, no decision raised |
| `[All Turns] When this Digimon suspends` | `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger event:"whenSuspended" sourceFilter:{isSelfRef:true}` | own turn: "de-digivolves an opposing stack when it suspends to attack" (public `attack`); opponent's turn: the blocker test (suspension by blocking); negative: "ignores another friendly Digimon suspending" |
| `＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon` | `DeDigivolve target:{controller:"opponent", kind:["Digimon"]} count 1, amount: 1` | stack fixture `BT1-015 over ST1-02`: top card instance id moves to the opponent trash, the under-card becomes the new top, `stack` empties |
| No-op boundary | same action | "resolves with no pending decision when the only opposing Digimon has no digivolution cards" — the bare Digimon stays, is not trashed, Ciel still suspends |
| `(Rule)` name alias `[Sistermon Noir]` | `Rule` → `GrantStatic grant:"name" tokens:["Sistermon Noir"]` | "carries the official (Rule) alternate name and Virus trait grant" (IR pin) and "answers to [Sistermon Noir] on the live continuous ledger" — `grantedNames` is exactly `["sistermon noir"]`, `effectiveNames` contains both Ciel and Noir and does **not** contain "sistermon blanc" (the exact-name negative) |
| `(Rule)` Virus attribute | `Rule` → `GrantStatic grant:"trait" tokens:["Virus"]` | "is seen by an opposing card's public [Virus] trait condition" — BT14-102 Angemon's `[When Attacking]` places an opposing `[Virus]`-trait Digimon at the bottom of security; Ciel is the card placed, the Vaccine peer is not |
| Peer name-axis boundary | consumed by BT20-013 `nameOrTrait [{tokens:["Sistermon","Gankoomon"], match:"name"}]` | new test "is selected by a peer card's public [Sistermon]-substring name reference…" — BaoHuckmon's `[Main]` plays Ciel from hand for 4−2 = 2 (memory 6→4); the plain Champion BT1-015 stays in hand |

### BT23-078 — Gorou Matayoshi

#### Contract and rules evidence

Catalog source: `packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` → `BT23-078`. Red Tamer, play cost 3,
trait CS.

Printed clauses:
1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[Your Turn] When your Digimon are played or digivolve, if any of them have [Avian], [Bird],
   [Beast], [Animal] or [Sovereign] in any of their traits (other than [Sea Animal]) or the [CS]
   trait, by returning this Tamer to the hand, 1 of your Digimon gets +3000 DP for the turn.
   Then, 1 of your Digimon may attack.`
3. `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-078`:
- Erratum (2025-10-17): `Goro Matayoshi` → `Gorou Matayoshi`, "Correction of official name".
  **The erratum is a name correction only — it changes no effect text.** The catalog `nameEn` is
  already the post-errata `Gorou Matayoshi`, and the test pins that value, so the module matches
  the errata and not the pre-errata printing. `data/kb/errata.json` confirms the single change.
- Q5353: the part after "then" cannot be processed without meeting the "by" condition — if you
  do not return the Tamer to the hand, the `[Your Turn]` effect will not let your Digimon attack.

Rules boundaries: "in any of their traits" is the substring axis (CR 2-3-2-4), so `[Giant Bird]`
counts as `[Bird]`; "the [CS] trait" is exact (CR 2-3-2-3). "1 of your Digimon" is the battle
area, never breeding (CR 3-4-5-8).

| `[Start of Your Main Phase] … gain 1 memory` | `StartOfYourMainPhase` → `GainMemory amount:1 condition:{kind:"opponentHas", filter:{controllerDefault:"opponent", kind:["Digimon"]}}` | "gains 1 memory at the start of its controller's Main phase when the opponent has a Digimon" — real `runTurn(0)`, exactly one `+1` memory event |
| Condition negative | same condition | "gains nothing at the start of Main while the opponent has no Digimon" (opponent holds only a Tamer) |
| Turn ownership | `StartOfYourMainPhase` | "stays silent at the start of the opponent's Main phase" — real turn loop into seat 1's Main, zero `+1` gains, Gorou still on seat 0's board |
| `[Your Turn] When your Digimon are played or digivolve` | two `SubTrigger`s, `whenPlayed` and `whenOneOfYoursDigivolves`, `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` | play route: "returns itself to hand, gives +3000 DP and lets a Digimon attack when a [Bird] Digimon is played"; digivolve route: "also fires when one of your Digimon digivolves into a [Beast] Digimon" (BT1-049 → BT12-036, memory 10→8, DP 4000→7000) |
| `[Avian]/[Bird]/[Beast]/[Animal]/[Sovereign] in any of their traits` | `nameOrTrait [{tokens:[…], match:"traitContains"}]` (substring) | "reads [Giant Bird] as [Bird] per CR 2-3-2-4" (BT1-014 Kokatorimon) |
| `(other than [Sea Animal])` | `excludeNameOrTrait [{tokens:["Sea Animal"], match:"trait"}]` (exact) | "ignores a [Sea Animal] Digimon…" (BT1-033 Dolphmon) — Gorou stays in play, ally DP unchanged, no attack |
| `or the [CS] trait` (second `or` branch) | `{nameOrTrait:[{tokens:["CS"], match:"trait"}]}` | "still fires on a [Sea Animal] Digimon that also has the [CS] trait" (BT23-023 Whamon): the exclusion is scoped to the trait-substring branch only, so the [CS] branch still qualifies |

| `1 of your Digimon gets +3000 DP for the turn` | `ModifyDP amount:3000 duration:"forTheTurn" target:{controller:"mine", zone:"battleArea", kind:["Digimon"]}` | ally 3000 → 6000; Whamon 9000 → 12000 |

| Controller boundary | `sourceFilter.controller: "mine"` on a `YourTurn` effect | "does not react while it is not its controller's turn and the played Digimon is not theirs" |
| `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.` | `Security` effect, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost payCost:false` | "[Security] plays itself into the battle area without paying its 3 cost" — real attack and security check; Gorou enters the battle area, is not trashed, memory unchanged at 3 |

| Turn ownership | `StartOfYourMainPhase` | "does not gain memory on the opponent's Main phase even with an opposing Digimon" — real turn loop: seat 0 opens at 1 (gain fired), passes; seat 1 opens at the plain passed-turn 3 |

| Controller and turn negative | same `sourceFilter` on a `YourTurn` effect | "does not fire for the opponent's own link on the opponent's turn" — reached through the real turn loop; Eri unsuspended, the opponent's host gains only its own 2000 link DP. This one test carries both the "not your turn" and the "subject is an opponent Digimon" boundaries |
| `by suspending this Tamer` | `cost:{kind:"suspend", target:{isSelfRef:true, isSelf:true}}`, `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | positive: `perm("eri").isSuspended === true`; "cannot pay the suspend cost when Eri is already suspended, so nothing happens" — the link still resolves (1000 + 2000), Eri's 3000 never lands, no `digivolved` event |
| `those Digimon get +3000 DP` | `ModifyDP amount:3000 target:{sourceRef:"triggerSubject", count:1}` | host 1000 + link 2000 + Eri 3000 = 6000; the bystander Digimon is unchanged, proving the boost targets the trigger subject and not a free choice |
| `until your opponent's turn ends` | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` (a real `EffectDurationRef`) | same test walks the turn loop: still 6000 through the opponent's Main, back to 3000 when seat 0's next Main opens |

Asserted: `digivolved` event with `mechanic: "appFusion"` and `cardId: "BT23-021"`, new top card id, `enteredByEffect`, stack `[oldTop, link]` bottom-most first, `linked` emptied, memory 5→4 (link declaration 1, App Fusion cost 0), exactly one evolution draw |
| `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.` | `Security` effect, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost payCost:false` | "plays itself from Security without paying its cost of 3" — a real attack and security check on the real turn loop, compared against a control run where the revealed card is BT1-009 (no Security effect): the memory lines match exactly, so the cost-3 Tamer charged nothing |

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start of Your Main Phase, [CS] gate | `GainMemory 1` with `youHave` + `zone: "battleArea"`, `match: "trait"` | "gains exactly 1 memory at the production start…" (with/without [CS], delta of exactly 1) and "ignores a CS Digimon that is only in the breeding area" — both public, through `startTurnLoop` |
| [On Play] free play, cost ≤ 5, [Hudie] | `PlayWithoutCost`, `playCostLte: 5`, `optional: true`, `from: ["hand"]` | "publicly plays for 4 and free-plays exactly one cost-5-or-lower Hudie Digimon" (BT23-037 played, BT23-032 cost 8 and BT1-028 left in hand, memory 6→2); "may decline the On Play free play" |
| "you may" optionality | `optional: true` | decline test above, `autoDeclineOptional` |
| [All Turns] Hudie suspends → suspend self → -3000 DP | `SubTrigger whenSuspended` + `ModifyDP -3000`, `duration: "forTheTurn"`, `cost: suspend self`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | "suspends this Tamer on a public Hudie attack…" (attack route, target 10000→7000); "may decline the suspension cost"; "does not trigger when a non-Hudie Digimon… suspends"; "cannot pay the cost when this Tamer is already suspended"; **new** "also fires on the opponent's turn when a Hudie blocker suspends" (BT23-050 Ankylomon blocks on seat 1's turn → 20000→17000) |
| "for the turn" duration | `duration: "forTheTurn"` (a real `EffectDurationRef`) | "expires the -3000 DP at the real turn boundary" (real turn loop into seat 1's Main) |
| [Security] | `Security` effect, `isSecurity: true`, `PlayWithoutCost` self | "plays itself from security without paying its 4 cost" (real attack, security check) |
| **[CS] trait is exact** | `match: "trait"` (exact), not `traitContains` | **new** "reads [CS] as an exact trait: an [Abadin Electronics] near-miss grants nothing" — "Abadin Electronics" contains "cs" as a substring, so a substring matcher would satisfy the gate; the gate is existential, so a wrongly matching near-miss flattens the asserted delta |
| **[On Play] mixed pool** | `playCostLte: 5` + trait [Hudie] over the whole hand | **new** "offers every eligible [Hudie] Digimon from a mixed hand and plays exactly the chosen one" — hand holds two qualifying (BT23-037 cost 3, BT23-040 cost 4), a right-trait/wrong-cost near-miss (BT23-032, cost 8) and a right-cost/wrong-trait near-miss (BT1-028); `preferInstanceIds` steers the selection to the **second** qualifying card, and the exact remaining hand is asserted |
| **Watcher reads the live top card** | `sourceFilter` evaluated against the permanent's top card | **new** "reads the current top card of a real two-step evolution stack, not its base" — BT1-047 Tinkermon (no [Hudie]) → BT23-041 Kabuterimon → BT23-032 Shakkoumon through two public `digivolve` intents (`useAlternateCost: true` takes the reduced "Lv.4 w/[CS]: Cost 3" route; memory 8→2), `Permanent.stack === [tinkermon, kabuterimon]`, then the attack fires the watcher off the [Hudie] top card |

### BT23-082 — Makiko Date

#### Contract and rules evidence
Catalog (`cardId: "BT23-082"`): Yellow Tamer, play cost 3, trait [CS].
Printed clauses:
1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] If your opponent has a Digimon, gain 1 memory.`
2. `[Your Turn] When any of your Digimon digivolve into a Digimon with the [Beastkin], [Holy Beast], [Cherub] or [CS] trait, by returning this Tamer to the hand, you may play 1 [Lopmon] or level 3 Digimon card with the [CS] trait from your hand without paying the cost.`
3. `securityEffectText`: `[Security] Play this card without paying the cost.`

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-082` → "(no knowledge-base entries)". KB-INDEX lists none.

**Exact-name review (coordinator's open item):** the printed `[Lopmon]` is a bracketed name, not "in its name", so it is exact. The IR uses `match: "nameExact"` (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/filters/cardPredicates.ts` documents `nameExact` as literal card-name equality). Verdict: correct as written, no change needed. The behavioural negative is BT16-067 "Lopmon (X Antibody)", which a substring match would wrongly accept.

#### Clause → IR → behavioral proof
| Printed clause | IR / implementation | Focused proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start of Your Main Phase, opponent has a Digimon | `GainMemory 1` with `opponentHas` | "gains 1 memory at the start of its controller's main phase while the opponent has a Digimon" (memory 0→1 through `startTurnLoop`); "gains no memory when the opponent controls no Digimon" |
| "[Start of YOUR Main Phase]" scope | trigger `StartOfYourMainPhase` | "stays silent at the opponent's own start of main phase" (exact 3 after the turn pass) |
| Watcher on digivolution into the 4 listed traits | `SubTrigger whenOneOfYoursDigivolves` with `sourceFilter.nameOrTrait tokens ["Beastkin","Holy Beast","Cherub","CS"], match "trait"` | [CS] positive: "returns itself to the hand to play a [Lopmon] for free after a CS digivolution" (BT23-041); **new** `it.each` positives for [Holy Beast] (BT1-051 Reppamon) and [Beastkin] (BT3-037 Turuiemon), both inert; negative: "does not fire for a digivolution into a Digimon without a listed trait" (BT5-038, [Mysterious Beast]) |
| Cost: return this Tamer to the hand | `cost: { kind: "return", to: "hand", isSelf }` | positives assert the Tamer instance leaves the battle area and appears in hand; the decline test asserts it stays on the board |
| "you may" | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` | "keeps the Tamer on the board and the card in hand when the option is declined" |
| Play 1 [Lopmon] **or** level 3 [CS] Digimon, free | `PlayWithoutCost` with `filter` (nameExact Lopmon) + `orFilters` (levels [3] + [CS] trait), `payCost: false` | "plays a level 3 Digimon with the [CS] trait and leaves an unrelated level 3 in hand" (BT22-043 played, BT1-009 left, memory unchanged by the play); "refuses a level 4 [CS] Digimon" (BT23-018); "refuses [Lopmon (X Antibody)]" (BT16-067) |
| [Security] | `Security` effect, `PlayWithoutCost` self | "plays itself for free from security" — **rewritten** to reach seat 1's Main through the real turn loop |
| Peer | — | "fires off BT23-026 Lopmon's Makiko-gated route into [Antylamon]" (a peer card whose alternate route is gated on this Tamer being in play) |

`node tools/kb/query.mjs card BT23-084` → **Q5257**, **Q5319**, **Q5357**.
- Q5357 (this card's own [End of Your Turn]): an [On Play] effect does not trigger for a card played into the breeding area. Covered behaviourally with BT23-006 Huckmon.
- Q5257 and Q5319 are the same ruling recorded twice, and the "this card" they resolve is **BT23-101 Hudiemon's** [On Play]/DNA digivolve line, not BT23-084's. BT23-084 appears only as the digivolution card that supplies the inherited ＜Alliance＞ and as the [Erika Mishima] digivolution source. The part that belongs to this card — Erika sitting in the digivolution cards, and Erika being a legal digivolution source — is proved below; the ＜Alliance＞-ordering half is BT23-101's contract.

The test's stale "catalog discrepancy" comment has been corrected; its assertion (join both fields, normalise whitespace) already survives either layout.

| "you may" | `optional: true` | "declining the optional pays neither cost and plays nothing" |
| "to your **empty** breeding area" | `requiresEmpty: "breedingArea"` | "pays nothing when the breeding area is occupied" (no suspension, no return) |
| Cost payable only with a [Hudie] Digimon | `cost.costs[1].filter` trait [Hudie] | "pays nothing without a [Hudie] Digimon to return" |

#### Retained reds and gaps
No `it.fails`. One open engine question for the coordinator, found while writing the new negative and **not** blessed by an assertion:

- **Seam:** `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/subTrigger.ts`, the activation-cost block around lines 1188–1215. It asks the "by suspending this Tamer" question and pays the cost **before** checking whether the body can do anything. With no eligible Option in hand (two-color or non-[CS]), an accepting player still suspends Ryuji for no effect. Expected, if CR 15-8-4-4-1's "only declare activation while the optional processing conditions can be performed" extends to trigger-type effects with a "by" cost: no prompt, no suspension. Actual: prompt raised, cost paid, nothing used. The reading is genuinely ambiguous for trigger-type effects (15-8-4 is written for activation-type [Main] effects) and no Q&A on this card settles it, so I neither asserted the current behaviour nor asserted the alternative; the new test asserts only that the two-color Option is never used, with a comment pointing here. The same gap sits under the pre-existing "does not use an Option without the [CS] trait" test, which also does not assert the Tamer's suspension state.

#### Catalog discrepancies
None affecting behaviour. `effectText` clause 2 ends without a period and with a trailing space, matching the official list.
